### [334] [Fusing Driver Perceived and Physical Risk for Safety Critical Scenario Screening in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20232)
*Chen Xiong,Ziwen Wang,Deqi Wang,Cheng Wang,Yiyang Chen,He Zhang,Chao Gou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于驾驶员风险融合的自动驾驶危险场景筛选方法，通过改进的驾驶员风险场和动态成本模型生成高质量风险监督信号，实现高效的大规模场景级风险评估和检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶测试从大规模自然驾驶数据中挖掘安全关键场景时，仍依赖人工风险标注和逐帧风险评估，导致效率低下且风险量化基础薄弱。

Method: 1) 改进的驾驶员风险场：引入新的风险高度函数和速度自适应前瞻机制；2) 动态成本模型：整合动能、定向边界框约束和高斯核扩散平滑；3) 风险轨迹交叉注意力解码器：联合解码风险和轨迹；训练时生成高质量风险监督信号，推理时直接预测场景级风险分数。

Result: 在INTERACTION和FLUID数据集上实验表明，该方法产生更平滑、更具区分度的风险估计。在FLUID数据集上，AUC达到0.792，AP达到0.825，分别比PODAR方法提升9.1%和5.1%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够实现可扩展的风险标注和危险场景筛选，为自动驾驶测试提供高效的大规模场景风险评估解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous driving testing increasingly relies on mining safety critical scenarios from large scale naturalistic driving data, yet existing screening pipelines still depend on manual risk annotation and expensive frame by frame risk evaluation, resulting in low efficiency and weakly grounded risk quantification. To address this issue, we propose a driver risk fusion based hazardous scenario screening method for autonomous driving. During training, the method combines an improved Driver Risk Field with a dynamic cost model to generate high quality risk supervision signals, while during inference it directly predicts scenario level risk scores through fast forward passes, avoiding per frame risk computation and enabling efficient large scale ranking and retrieval. The improved Driver Risk Field introduces a new risk height function and a speed adaptive look ahead mechanism, and the dynamic cost model integrates kinetic energy, oriented bounding box constraints, and Gaussian kernel diffusion smoothing for more accurate interaction modeling. We further design a risk trajectory cross attention decoder to jointly decode risk and trajectories. Experiments on the INTERACTION and FLUID datasets show that the proposed method produces smoother and more discriminative risk estimates. On FLUID, it achieves an AUC of 0.792 and an AP of 0.825, outperforming PODAR by 9.1 percent and 5.1 percent, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness for scalable risk labeling and hazardous scenario screening.

</details>


### [335] [SwiftBot: A Decentralized Platform for LLM-Powered Federated Robotic Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20233)
*YueMing Zhang,Shuai Xu,Zhengxiong Li,Fangtian Zhong,Xiaokun Yang,Hailu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwiftBot是一个联邦机器人任务执行平台，通过LLM任务分解和智能容器编排，在DHT覆盖网络上实现去中心化协作，显著提升任务执行效率和资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦机器人任务执行系统存在三个主要问题：1) 需要大量领域工程的手动编码规划器；2) 集中式协调与联邦协作理念相悖；3) 静态资源分配无法适应动态工作负载变化。需要一种既能理解自然语言指令，又能高效管理异构边缘设备资源的解决方案。

Method: SwiftBot整合了基于LLM的任务分解和智能容器编排技术，通过DHT覆盖网络实现去中心化协作。系统能够将自然语言指令分解为可执行任务，并通过联邦热容器迁移机制动态分配计算资源，实现无中心控制的机器人协作。

Result: SwiftBot在多样化任务上达到94.3%的分解准确率，任务启动延迟降低1.5-5.4倍，平均训练延迟降低1.4-2.5倍，高负载下尾部延迟改善1.2-4.7倍。多媒体任务评估验证了语义理解和联邦资源管理的协同设计效果。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计语义理解和联邦资源管理，SwiftBot实现了灵活高效的机器人任务控制，为联邦机器人系统提供了既具备自然语言理解能力又能动态管理计算资源的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated robotic task execution systems require bridging natural language instructions to distributed robot control while efficiently managing computational resources across heterogeneous edge devices without centralized coordination. Existing approaches face three limitations: rigid hand-coded planners requiring extensive domain engineering, centralized coordination that contradicts federated collaboration as robots scale, and static resource allocation failing to share containers across robots when workloads shift dynamically. We present SwiftBot, a federated task execution platform that integrates LLM-based task decomposition with intelligent container orchestration over a DHT overlay, enabling robots to collaboratively execute tasks without centralized control. SwiftBot achieves 94.3% decomposition accuracy across diverse tasks, reduces task startup latency by 1.5-5.4x and average training latency by 1.4-2.5x, and improves tail latency by 1.2-4.7x under high load through federated warm container migration. Evaluation on multimedia tasks validates that co-designing semantic understanding and federated resource management enables both flexibility and efficiency for robotic task control.

</details>


### [336] [Emergency Lane-Change Simulation: A Behavioral Guidance Approach for Risky Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20234)
*Chen Xiong,Cheng Wang,Yuhang Liu,Zirui Wu,Ye Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于行为引导的高风险变道场景生成方法，通过优化序列生成对抗网络学习紧急变道行为，结合递归近端策略优化探索危险场景，比传统网格搜索和人工设计更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶测试中，虚拟仿真因其高效性和成本效益成为重要方法，但现有方法通常依赖强化学习生成风险场景，难以高效学习真实的紧急行为。

Method: 1) 基于优化序列生成对抗网络的行为学习模块，从提取的数据集中学习紧急变道行为；2) 将对抗车辆建模为智能体，将道路环境和周围车辆纳入操作环境；3) 基于递归近端策略优化策略，使用生成的轨迹引导车辆走向危险行为；4) 结合参考轨迹和模型预测控制作为物理约束，持续优化策略并确保物理真实性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够从有限数据中有效学习高风险轨迹行为，并比传统网格搜索和人工设计方法更高效地生成高风险碰撞场景。

Conclusion: 提出的行为引导方法能够有效解决现有自动驾驶测试中紧急行为学习效率低的问题，为高风险场景生成提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In contemporary autonomous driving testing, virtual simulation has become an important approach due to its efficiency and cost effectiveness. However, existing methods usually rely on reinforcement learning to generate risky scenarios, making it difficult to efficiently learn realistic emergency behaviors. To address this issue, we propose a behavior guided method for generating high risk lane change scenarios. First, a behavior learning module based on an optimized sequence generative adversarial network is developed to learn emergency lane change behaviors from an extracted dataset. This design alleviates the limitations of existing datasets and improves learning from relatively few samples. Then, the opposing vehicle is modeled as an agent, and the road environment together with surrounding vehicles is incorporated into the operating environment. Based on the Recursive Proximal Policy Optimization strategy, the generated trajectories are used to guide the vehicle toward dangerous behaviors for more effective risk scenario exploration. Finally, the reference trajectory is combined with model predictive control as physical constraints to continuously optimize the strategy and ensure physical authenticity. Experimental results show that the proposed method can effectively learn high risk trajectory behaviors from limited data and generate high risk collision scenarios with better efficiency than traditional methods such as grid search and manual design.

</details>


### [337] [EnergyAction: Unimanual to Bimanual Composition with Energy-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20236)
*Mingchen Song,Xiang Deng,Jie Wei,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EnergyAction框架，通过能量模型将单臂操作策略组合为双臂策略，解决双臂操作数据不足和协调困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单臂操作策略已取得显著成功，但扩展到双臂操作面临挑战：缺乏双臂演示数据，且双臂动作协调复杂。现有方法要么依赖大量双臂数据集，要么未能有效利用预训练的单臂策略。

Method: 1. 将单臂策略建模为能量模型，利用其组合特性融合左右臂动作；2. 通过能量约束引入时空协调机制；3. 提出两种基于能量的动态去噪策略，根据动作质量评估自适应调整去噪步数。

Result: 实验表明，EnergyAction能有效将单臂知识迁移到双臂任务，在仿真和真实世界任务中均取得优异性能，且仅需少量双臂数据。

Conclusion: EnergyAction框架通过能量模型实现了单臂策略向双臂任务的有效迁移，解决了双臂操作中的数据不足和协调问题，为机器人双臂操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in unimanual manipulation policies have achieved remarkable success across diverse robotic tasks through abundant training data and well-established model architectures. However, extending these capabilities to bimanual manipulation remains challenging due to the lack of bimanual demonstration data and the complexity of coordinating dual-arm actions. Existing approaches either rely on extensive bimanual datasets or fail to effectively leverage pre-trained unimanual policies. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{EnergyAction}, a novel framework that compositionally transfers unimanual manipulation policies to bimanual tasks through the Energy-Based Models (EBMs). Specifically, our method incorporates three key innovations. First, we model individual unimanual policies as EBMs and leverage their compositional properties to compose left and right arm actions, enabling the fusion of unimanual policies into a bimanual policy. Second, we introduce an energy-based temporal-spatial coordination mechanism through energy constraints, ensuring the generated bimanual actions are both temporal coherence and spatial feasibility. Third, we propose two different energy-aware denoising strategies that dynamically adapt denoising steps based on action quality assessment. These strategies ensure the generation of high-quality actions while maintaining superior computational efficiency compared to fixed-step denoising approaches. Experimental results demonstrate that EnergyAction effectively transfers unimanual knowledge to bimanual tasks, achieving superior performance on both simulated and real-world tasks with minimal bimanual data.

</details>


### [338] [Rheos: Modelling Continuous Motion Dynamics in Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20239)
*Iacopo Catalano,Francesco Verdoja,Javier Civera,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Julio A. Placed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rheos框架将连续方向运动模型嵌入3D场景图的动态层，使用半包裹高斯混合模型替代离散直方图，通过储层采样和BIC优化实现高效在线操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图对动态建模有限，仅跟踪单个智能体；动态地图依赖均匀网格离散化，缺乏语义基础且扩展性差。需要结合语义层次结构与连续动态建模。

Method: 在分层3D场景图中添加动态层，每个动态节点维护半包裹高斯混合模型捕捉多模态方向流；使用储层采样进行有界内存观测缓冲，并行单元模型更新，基于BIC准则选择最优混合分量数。

Result: 在模拟行人环境中四个空间分辨率下评估，Rheos在连续和不利离散指标下均优于离散基线方法；将每次更新初始化成本从样本数的二次方降至线性。

Conclusion: Rheos成功将连续动态模型嵌入3D场景图，提供有原则的概率分布表示，支持高效在线操作，优于传统离散方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) provide hierarchical, multi-resolution abstractions that encode the geometric and semantic structure of an environment, yet their treatment of dynamics remains limited to tracking individual agents. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) complement this by modeling aggregate motion patterns, but rely on uniform grid discretizations that lack semantic grounding and scale poorly. We present Rheos, a framework that explicitly embeds continuous directional motion models into an additional dynamics layer of a hierarchical 3DSG that enhances the navigational properties of the graph. Each dynamics node maintains a semi-wrapped Gaussian mixture model that captures multimodal directional flow as a principled probability distribution with explicit uncertainty, replacing the discrete histograms used in prior work. To enable online operation, Rheos employs reservoir sampling for bounded-memory observation buffers, parallel per-cell model updates and a principled Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) sweep that selects the optimal number of mixture components, reducing per-update initialization cost from quadratic to linear in the number of samples. Evaluated across four spatial resolutions in a simulated pedestrian environment, Rheos consistently outperforms the discrete baseline under continuous as well as unfavorable discrete metrics. We release our implementation as open source.

</details>


### [339] [TRGS-SLAM: IMU-Aided Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Blurry, Rolling Shutter, and Noisy Thermal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20443)
*Spencer Carmichael,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRGS-SLAM：首个基于3D高斯泼溅的视觉惯性热成像SLAM系统，能够处理热成像特有的运动模糊、卷帘快门和固定模式噪声，在恶劣视觉条件下实现鲁棒定位与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热成像相机在机器人SLAM中具有独特优势：被动式、低功耗、可在黑暗环境工作、对光照变化不敏感、能穿透雾/尘/烟。但常用的非制冷微测辐射热计热成像相机存在严重运动模糊、卷帘快门畸变和固定模式噪声，现有SLAM方法难以处理这些退化问题。

Method: 提出TRGS-SLAM系统，基于3D高斯泼溅框架，引入模型感知的3DGS渲染方法处理热成像数据退化。创新包括：采用B样条轨迹优化的两阶段IMU损失、基于视图多样性的不透明度重置策略、姿态漂移校正方案。

Result: 系统在真实世界快速运动、高噪声热成像数据上实现精确跟踪，而其他测试的SLAM方法均失败。通过离线优化SLAM结果，实现了与需要真实姿态的先验工作相竞争的热图像恢复质量。

Conclusion: TRGS-SLAM是首个能够有效处理热成像相机特有退化的3DGS SLAM系统，在恶劣视觉条件下实现了鲁棒的定位与建图，并通过离线优化展示了高质量的热图像恢复能力。

Abstract: Thermal cameras offer several advantages for simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) with mobile robots: they provide a passive, low-power solution to operating in darkness, are invariant to rapidly changing or high dynamic range illumination, and can see through fog, dust, and smoke. However, uncooled microbolometer thermal cameras, the only practical option in most robotics applications, suffer from significant motion blur, rolling shutter distortions, and fixed pattern noise. In this paper, we present TRGS-SLAM, a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) based thermal inertial SLAM system uniquely capable of handling these degradations. To overcome the challenges of thermal data, we introduce a model-aware 3DGS rendering method and several general innovations to 3DGS SLAM, including B-spline trajectory optimization with a two-stage IMU loss, view-diversity-based opacity resetting, and pose drift correction schemes. Our system demonstrates accurate tracking on real-world, fast motion, and high-noise thermal data that causes all other tested SLAM methods to fail. Moreover, through offline refinement of our SLAM results, we demonstrate thermal image restoration competitive with prior work that required ground truth poses.

</details>


### [340] [An Open Source Computer Vision and Machine Learning Framework for Affordable Life Science Robotic Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20465)
*Zachary Logan,Andrew Dudash,Daniel Negrón*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源机器人框架集成计算机视觉和机器学习逆运动学，用于低成本实验室自动化任务如菌落挑选和液体处理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发低成本、开源的实验室自动化解决方案，替代昂贵的商业系统，使更多实验室能够实现自动化操作

Method: 使用自定义训练的U-net模型进行微生物培养物的语义分割，结合混合密度网络预测5自由度机器人臂的关节角度，并配备定制液体处理末端执行器

Result: 系统实现精确可重复操作：平均位置误差低于1mm，关节角度预测误差低于4度，菌落检测IoU得分为0.537，Dice系数为0.596

Conclusion: 该框架证明了低成本实验室自动化的可行性，为微生物学、合成生物学等领域的自动化操作提供了经济有效的解决方案

Abstract: We present an open-source robotic framework that integrates computer vision and machine learning based inverse kinematics to enable low-cost laboratory automation tasks such as colony picking and liquid handling. The system uses a custom trained U-net model for semantic segmentation of microbial cultures, combined with Mixture Density Network for predicating joint angles of a simple 5-DOF robot arm. We evaluated the framework using a modified robot arm, upgraded with a custom liquid handling end-effector. Experimental results demonstrate the framework's feasibility for precise, repeatable operations, with mean positional error below 1 mm and joint angle prediction errors below 4 degrees and colony detection capabilities with IoU score of 0.537 and Dice coefficient of 0.596.

</details>


### [341] [High-Speed, All-Terrain Autonomy: Ensuring Safety at the Limits of Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20525)
*James R. Baxter,Bogdan I. Epureanu,Paramsothy Jayakumar,Tulga Ersal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新型局部轨迹规划器，能够控制自动驾驶越野车在崎岖地形高速行驶，通过能量约束防止侧翻，实现实时计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶车辆无法在崎岖地形高速安全行驶，现有方法要么无法预测和防止地形引起的侧翻，要么无法实时计算

Method: 开发新型模型预测控制(MPC)公式，推导出用于崎岖非平面地形的车辆动力学模型，引入基于能量的约束来安全实现极端机动（包括轮胎离地但不侧翻），通过并行GPGPU计算实现实时性

Result: 在模拟和实际实验中验证了规划器提供安全极端轨迹的能力，相比最先进基准方法，在多个挑战性场景中实现了更少的侧翻和更高的成功率

Conclusion: 该规划器能够安全控制自动驾驶越野车在崎岖地形高速行驶，解决了现有方法在侧翻预防和实时性方面的不足，为极端越野机动提供了可行方案

Abstract: A novel local trajectory planner, capable of controlling an autonomous off-road vehicle on rugged terrain at high-speed is presented. Autonomous vehicles are currently unable to safely operate off-road at high-speed, as current approaches either fail to predict and mitigate rollovers induced by rough terrain or are not real-time feasible. To address this challenge, a novel model predictive control (MPC) formulation is developed for local trajectory planning. A new dynamics model for off-road vehicles on rough, non-planar terrain is derived and used for prediction. Extreme mobility, including tire liftoff without rollover, is safely enabled through a new energy-based constraint. The formulation is analytically shown to mitigate rollover types ignored by many state-of-the-art methods, and real-time feasibility is achieved through parallelized GPGPU computation. The planner's ability to provide safe, extreme trajectories is studied through both simulated trials and full-scale physical experiments. The results demonstrate fewer rollovers and more successes compared to a state-of-the-art baseline across several challenging scenarios that push the vehicle to its mobility limits.

</details>


### [342] [Memory Over Maps: 3D Object Localization Without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20530)
*Rui Zhou,Xander Yap,Jianwen Cao,Allison Lau,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需构建完整3D场景重建的目标定位方法，仅存储RGB-D关键帧作为轻量视觉记忆，通过视觉语言模型检索和重排序实现目标定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于3D重建的目标定位方法存在映射时间长、存储开销大、可扩展性差的问题，而视觉语言模型的发展使得直接在2D观测上进行语义推理成为可能

Method: 存储带位姿的RGB-D关键帧作为视觉记忆，查询时通过检索候选视图、视觉语言模型重排序、深度反投影和多视角融合构建稀疏的按需3D估计

Result: 相比重建方法，预处理成本大幅降低，场景索引构建速度快两个数量级，存储需求显著减少，在物体目标导航任务中表现良好

Conclusion: 基于图像的场景记忆直接推理可以有效替代密集3D重建，用于以物体为中心的机器人导航任务

Abstract: Target localization is a prerequisite for embodied tasks such as navigation and manipulation. Conventional approaches rely on constructing explicit 3D scene representations to enable target localization, such as point clouds, voxel grids, or scene graphs. While effective, these pipelines incur substantial mapping time, storage overhead, and scalability limitations. Recent advances in vision-language models suggest that rich semantic reasoning can be performed directly on 2D observations, raising a fundamental question: is a complete 3D scene reconstruction necessary for object localization? In this work, we revisit object localization and propose a map-free pipeline that stores only posed RGB-D keyframes as a lightweight visual memory--without constructing any global 3D representation of the scene. At query time, our method retrieves candidate views, re-ranks them with a vision-language model, and constructs a sparse, on-demand 3D estimate of the queried target through depth backprojection and multi-view fusion. Compared to reconstruction-based pipelines, this design drastically reduces preprocessing cost, enabling scene indexing that is over two orders of magnitude faster to build while using substantially less storage. We further validate the localized targets on downstream object-goal navigation tasks. Despite requiring no task-specific training, our approach achieves strong performance across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating that direct reasoning over image-based scene memory can effectively replace dense 3D reconstruction for object-centric robot navigation. Project page: https://ruizhou-cn.github.io/memory-over-maps/

</details>


### [343] [Multi-Robot Learning-Informed Task Planning Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20544)
*Abhish Khanal,Abhishek Paudel,Hung Pham,Gregory J. Stein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多机器人规划抽象方法，结合学习估计环境不确定性与基于模型的长期协调规划，在未知物体位置的任务环境中实现高效多阶段任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队需要在未知任务相关物体位置的环境中最小化完成复杂任务的时间。现有方法面临挑战：即使部分信息已知，协调哪个机器人何时执行哪个动作也很困难；不确定性导致每个动作有多种可能结果，进一步复杂化长期决策和协调。

Method: 提出多机器人规划抽象方法，整合学习组件来估计环境的不确定方面，同时使用基于模型的规划进行长期协调。该方法在ProcTHOR家庭环境中进行测试，并扩展到真实环境中的LoCoBot移动机器人团队。

Result: 在ProcTHOR大型家庭环境中，该方法在1、2、3个机器人团队上表现出优于竞争基线的多阶段任务规划效率。同时，在真实家庭环境中，两个LoCoBot移动机器人团队也验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的学习与规划结合的多机器人抽象方法能够有效处理环境不确定性，实现长期协调规划，在仿真和真实环境中都展示了高效的多机器人任务完成能力。

Abstract: We want a multi-robot team to complete complex tasks in minimum time where the locations of task-relevant objects are not known. Effective task completion requires reasoning over long horizons about the likely locations of task-relevant objects, how individual actions contribute to overall progress, and how to coordinate team efforts. Planning in this setting is extremely challenging: even when task-relevant information is partially known, coordinating which robot performs which action and when is difficult, and uncertainty introduces a multiplicity of possible outcomes for each action, which further complicates long-horizon decision-making and coordination. To address this, we propose a multi-robot planning abstraction that integrates learning to estimate uncertain aspects of the environment with model-based planning for long-horizon coordination. We demonstrate the efficient multi-stage task planning of our approach for 1, 2, and 3 robot teams over competitive baselines in large ProcTHOR household environments. Additionally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach with a team of two LoCoBot mobile robots in real household settings.

</details>


### [344] [Current state of the multi-agent multi-view experimental and digital twin rendezvous (MMEDR-Autonomous) framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20575)
*Logan Banker,Michael Wozniak,Mohanad Alameer,Smriti Nandan Paul,David Meisinger,Grant Baer,Trevor Hunting,Ryan Dunham,Jay Kamdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MMEDR-Autonomous框架，结合学习型光学导航网络、强化学习制导方法和硬件在环测试平台，用于自主交会对接任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着近地空间物体增多，在轨服务、碎片清除和轨道修改等应用需求增加，需要更自主的交会对接技术来降低操作复杂性和人工工作量。

Method: 1) 轻量级单目姿态估计网络，采用多尺度特征融合和真实图像增强训练；2) 强化学习制导方法，关注学习稳定性、奖励设计和超参数调优；3) 硬件在环测试平台；4) 基于Control Barrier Function的安全约束方法。

Result: 目前框架正在向多智能体交会场景的集成实验验证推进，导航网络已通过真实图像增强训练缓解域偏移问题，制导组件正在开发中。

Conclusion: MMEDR-Autonomous框架为自主交会对接提供了统一解决方案，结合了学习型导航、强化学习制导和硬件验证，有望提高空间操作自主性。

Abstract: As near-Earth resident space objects proliferate, there is an increasing demand for reliable technologies in applications of on-orbit servicing, debris removal, and orbit modification. Rendezvous and docking are critical mission phases for such applications and can benefit from greater autonomy to reduce operational complexity and human workload. Machine learning-based methods can be integrated within the guidance, navigation, and control (GNC) architecture to design a robust rendezvous and docking framework. In this work, the Multi-Agent Multi-View Experimental and Digital Twin Rendezvous (MMEDR-Autonomous) is introduced as a unified framework comprising a learning-based optical navigation network, a reinforcement learning-based guidance approach under ongoing development, and a hardware-in-the-loop testbed. Navigation employs a lightweight monocular pose estimation network with multi-scale feature fusion, trained on realistic image augmentations to mitigate domain shift. The guidance component is examined with emphasis on learning stability, reward design, and systematic hyperparameter tuning under mission-relevant constraints. Prior Control Barrier Function results for Clohessy-Wiltshire dynamics are reviewed as a basis for enforcing safety and operational constraints and for guiding future nonlinear controller design within the MMEDR-Autonomous framework. The MMEDR-Autonomous framework is currently progressing toward integrated experimental validation in multi-agent rendezvous scenarios.

</details>


### [345] [LASER: Level-Based Asynchronous Scheduling and Execution Regime for Spatiotemporally Constrained Multi-Robot Timber Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20577)
*Zhenxiang Huang,Lior Skoury,Tim Stark,Aaron Wagner,Hans Jakob Wagner,Thomas Wortmann,Achim Menges*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LASER框架通过层级化异步调度解决多机器人制造中的时空约束问题，在木材板制造中成功协调108个子程序和352个螺钉


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模制造（如木材建筑）需要多机器人系统处理紧密耦合的时空约束（如碰撞避免和过程驱动的时间限制），现有方法难以有效协调这些复杂任务

Method: 提出LASER框架，将屏障机制集成到约束编程调度中，将任务划分为时空不相交的层级；为大规模问题设计两种专门算法：异构任务序列的迭代时间松弛方法和同质任务的双层分解方法

Result: 成功制造了2.4m x 6m的全尺寸木材板，协调了108个子程序和352个螺钉；计算研究表明相比整体方法，该方法具有稳定的可扩展性

Conclusion: LASER框架通过层级化异步调度有效解决了多机器人制造中的时空约束问题，实现了安全、高效的并行操作，为大规模自动化制造提供了可行方案

Abstract: Automating large-scale manufacturing in domains like timber construction requires multi-robot systems to manage tightly coupled spatiotemporal constraints, such as collision avoidance and process-driven deadlines. This paper introduces LASER (Level-based Asynchronous Scheduling and Execution Regime), a complete framework for scheduling and executing complex assembly tasks, demonstrated on a screw-press gluing application for timber slab manufacturing. Our central contribution is to integrate a barrier-based mechanism into a constraint programming (CP) scheduling formulation that partitions tasks into spatiotemporally disjoint sets, which we define as levels. This structure enables robots to execute tasks in parallel and asynchronously within a level, synchronizing only at level barriers, which guarantees collision-free operation by construction and provides robustness to timing uncertainties. To solve this formulation for large problems, we propose two specialized algorithms: an iterative temporal-relaxation approach for heterogeneous task sequences and a bi-level decomposition for homogeneous tasks that balances workload. We validate the LASER framework by fabricating a full-scale 2.4m x 6m timber slab with a two-robot system mounted on parallel linear tracks, successfully coordinating 108 subroutines and 352 screws under tight adhesive time windows. Computational studies show our method scales steadily with size compared to a monolithic approach.

</details>


### [346] [Unified Orbit-Attitude Estimation and Sensor Tasking Framework for Autonomous Cislunar Space Domain Awareness Using Multiplicative Unscented Kalman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20579)
*Smriti Nandan Paul,Siwei Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个先进的地月空间态势感知框架，包含观测架构优化和传感器任务调度优化，解决地月空间非线性动力学带来的状态估计挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间存在强烈的非线性、非开普勒动力学特性，严重影响不确定性传播和状态估计精度。此外还有长距离观测需求、传感器-目标几何限制、照明条件、广阔监测区域以及传感器布局设计空间大等挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：(1) 基于Tree of Parzen Estimators算法的观测架构优化，考虑实际成本函数；(2) 基于互信息的传感器任务调度优化，结合误差状态乘法无迹卡尔曼滤波器进行轨道和姿态状态估计。

Result: 任务1的观测架构比随机搜索基线方案成本函数值显著降低且使用更少传感器。任务2显示平移状态估计在各种目标-观测器比例下表现良好，但姿态估计对目标-观测器比例和任务间隔更敏感，高目标数量和低频更新时旋转状态会发散。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了传感资源、任务调度频率和可达到的状态估计性能之间的重要权衡，这些因素影响自主地月空间态势感知的可扩展性。

Abstract: The cislunar regime departs from near-Earth orbital behavior through strongly non-linear, non-Keplerian dynamics, which adversely affect the accuracy of uncertainty propagation and state estimation. Additional challenges arise from long-range observation requirements, restrictive sensor-target geometry and illumination conditions, the need to monitor an expansive cislunar volume, and the large design space associated with space/ground-based sensor placement. In response to these challenges, this work introduces an advanced framework for cislunar space domain awareness (SDA) encompassing two key tasks: (1) observer architecture optimization based on a realistic cost formulation that captures key performance trade-offs, solved using the Tree of Parzen Estimators algorithm, and (2) leveraging the resulting observer architecture, a mutual information-driven sensor tasking optimization is performed at discrete tasking intervals, while orbital and attitude state estimation is carried out at a finer temporal resolution between successive tasking updates using an error-state multiplicative unscented Kalman filter. Numerical simulations demonstrate that our approach in Task 1 yields observer architectures that achieve significantly lower values of the proposed cost function than baseline random-search solutions, while using fewer sensors. Task 2 results show that translational state estimation remains satisfactory over a wide range of target-to-observer count ratios, whereas attitude estimation is significantly more sensitive to target-to-observer ratios and tasking intervals, with increased rotational-state divergence observed for high target counts and infrequent tasking updates. These results highlight important trade-offs between sensing resources, tasking cadence, and achievable state estimation performance that influence the scalability of autonomous cislunar SDA.

</details>


### [347] [GHOST: Ground-projected Hypotheses from Observed Structure-from-Motion Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20583)
*Tomasz Frelek,Rohan Patil,Akshar Tumu,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自监督学习的可扩展方法，从单目图像中分割可行车辆轨迹，利用大规模行车记录视频的自身运动作为隐式监督，无需人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在复杂城市环境中需要可靠地预测可行轨迹，但传统方法依赖道路标记的显式建模，难以适应多样化的真实世界场景。需要一种能够从大规模无约束数据中学习、无需人工标注的通用轨迹预测方法。

Method: 利用大规模行车记录视频，将记录的自身车辆运动作为隐式监督，通过单目结构从运动中恢复相机轨迹，将其投影到地面平面生成经过区域的空间掩码。使用这些自动生成的标签训练深度分割网络，从单张RGB图像预测运动条件路径建议，无需显式建模道路或车道标记。

Result: 在NuScenes数据集上评估显示可靠的轨迹预测性能。模型能够隐式捕捉场景布局、车道拓扑和交叉口结构，并能泛化到不同的相机配置。通过轻量微调可迁移到电动滑板车平台，表明大规模自身运动蒸馏能够产生结构化且可泛化的路径建议。

Conclusion: 大规模自身运动蒸馏能够产生结构化且可泛化的路径建议，超越了已演示的轨迹范围，通过图像分割实现轨迹假设估计。该方法为自动驾驶轨迹预测提供了一种可扩展的自监督解决方案。

Abstract: We present a scalable self-supervised approach for segmenting feasible vehicle trajectories from monocular images for autonomous driving in complex urban environments. Leveraging large-scale dashcam videos, we treat recorded ego-vehicle motion as implicit supervision and recover camera trajectories via monocular structure-from-motion, projecting them onto the ground plane to generate spatial masks of traversed regions without manual annotation. These automatically generated labels are used to train a deep segmentation network that predicts motion-conditioned path proposals from a single RGB image at run time, without explicit modeling of road or lane markings. Trained on diverse, unconstrained internet data, the model implicitly captures scene layout, lane topology, and intersection structure, and generalizes across varying camera configurations. We evaluate our approach on NuScenes, demonstrating reliable trajectory prediction, and further show transfer to an electric scooter platform through light fine-tuning. Our results indicate that large-scale ego-motion distillation yields structured and generalizable path proposals beyond the demonstrated trajectory, enabling trajectory hypothesis estimation via image segmentation.

</details>


### [348] [Towards Practical World Model-based Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20607)
*Zhilong Zhang,Haoxiang Ren,Yihao Sun,Yifei Sheng,Haonan Wang,Haoxin Lin,Zhichao Wu,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-MBPO：一个通过交互式世界模型微调VLA模型的实用框架，解决了像素级世界建模、多视图一致性和稀疏奖励下的误差累积问题，显著提升了策略性能和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLA模型在机器人控制中表现出强大的泛化能力，但使用强化学习进行微调受到真实世界交互的高成本和安全性风险的限制。在交互式世界模型中训练可以避免这些问题，但引入了像素级世界建模、多视图一致性和稀疏奖励下的误差累积等挑战。

Method: VLA-MBPO框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 采用统一多模态模型进行数据高效的世界建模；(2) 交错视图解码机制来强制多视图一致性；(3) 块级分支展开以减轻误差累积。

Result: 理论分析和仿真及真实世界任务的实验表明，VLA-MBPO显著提高了策略性能和样本效率，证明了其在真实世界机器人部署中的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VLA-MBPO为解决VLA模型微调中的关键挑战提供了一个实用框架，通过创新的世界建模和误差控制机制，实现了在安全、高效的环境中进行机器人策略优化。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show strong generalization for robotic control, but finetuning them with reinforcement learning (RL) is constrained by the high cost and safety risks of real-world interaction. Training VLA models in interactive world models avoids these issues but introduces several challenges, including pixel-level world modeling, multi-view consistency, and compounding errors under sparse rewards. Building on recent advances across large multimodal models and model-based RL, we propose VLA-MBPO, a practical framework to tackle these problems in VLA finetuning. Our approach has three key design choices: (i) adapting unified multimodal models (UMMs) for data-efficient world modeling; (ii) an interleaved view decoding mechanism to enforce multi-view consistency; and (iii) chunk-level branched rollout to mitigate error compounding. Theoretical analysis and experiments across simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that VLA-MBPO significantly improves policy performance and sample efficiency, underscoring its robustness and scalability for real-world robotic deployment.

</details>


### [349] [Speedup Patch: Learning a Plug-and-Play Policy to Accelerate Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20658)
*Zhichao Wu,Junyin Ye,Zhilong Zhang,Yihao Sun,Haoxin Lin,Jiaheng Luo,Haoxiang Ren,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuP是一个轻量级、策略无关的加速框架，通过外部调度器自适应降采样动作块来消除冗余，使用离线数据实现即插即用加速，在保持成功率的同时达到1.8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身策略虽然表现出显著的操控能力，但执行速度过慢，继承了人类演示的缓慢节奏。现有加速方法通常需要策略重新训练或昂贵的在线交互，限制了大规模基础模型的可扩展性。

Method: 提出Speedup Patch (SuP)框架，引入外部调度器自适应降采样动作块。将调度器优化形式化为约束马尔可夫决策过程(CMDP)，使用世界模型的状态偏差作为替代指标来强制执行安全约束，通过离线强化学习优化调度器。

Result: 在仿真基准测试(Libero, Bigym)和真实世界任务中，SuP为多样化策略实现了整体1.8倍的执行加速，同时保持了原始成功率。

Conclusion: SuP提供了一个轻量级、策略无关的即插即用加速解决方案，仅使用离线数据就能显著提高具身策略的执行速度，同时保持任务性能，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: While current embodied policies exhibit remarkable manipulation skills, their execution remains unsatisfactorily slow as they inherit the tardy pacing of human demonstrations. Existing acceleration methods typically require policy retraining or costly online interactions, limiting their scalability for large-scale foundation models. In this paper, we propose Speedup Patch (SuP), a lightweight, policy-agnostic framework that enables plug-and-play acceleration using solely offline data. SuP introduces an external scheduler that adaptively downsamples action chunks provided by embodied policies to eliminate redundancies. Specifically, we formalize the optimization of our scheduler as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) aimed at maximizing efficiency without compromising task performance. Since direct success evaluation is infeasible in offline settings, SuP introduces World Model based state deviation as a surrogate metric to enforce safety constraints. By leveraging a learned world model as a virtual evaluator to predict counterfactual trajectories, the scheduler can be optimized via offline reinforcement learning. Empirical results on simulation benchmarks (Libero, Bigym) and real-world tasks validate that SuP achieves an overall 1.8x execution speedup for diverse policies while maintaining their original success rates.

</details>


### [350] [StageCraft: Execution Aware Mitigation of Distractor and Obstruction Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20659)
*Kartikay Milind Pangaonkar,Prabin Rath,Omkar Patil,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StageCraft：一种无需训练的方法，利用大型视觉语言模型（VLM）的上下文推理能力，通过操纵环境初始状态来提升预训练视觉语言动作模型（VLA）在存在干扰物和障碍物时的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言动作模型（VLA）虽然在文本和图像预训练以及多样化机器人演示的帮助下能够泛化到新任务、新物体和新场景，但在执行时遇到干扰物和物理障碍等阻碍时仍然容易失败。现有的策略改进方法通过对基础VLA进行微调来提升泛化能力，但在未见过的干扰物设置中仍然表现不佳。

Method: StageCraft是一种无需训练的即插即用模块，它利用大型视觉语言模型（VLM）的上下文推理能力来分析策略执行视频和成功标签，推断出初始状态中哪些物体需要被操纵以避免预期的执行失败，从而在策略执行前对环境初始状态进行预处理。

Result: 在三个涉及多样化干扰物和障碍物的真实世界任务领域中，StageCraft使最先进的VLA模型性能提升了40%。在RLBench的模拟实验中，StageCraft能够根据底层策略的强度调整干预程度，并通过更多上下文样本进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: StageCraft展示了利用互联网规模预训练的大型视觉语言模型来推理执行时阻碍并缓解策略失败的潜力，为提升机器人策略在复杂环境中的鲁棒性提供了一种无需训练、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large scale pre-training on text and image data along with diverse robot demonstrations has helped Vision Language Action models (VLAs) to generalize to novel tasks, objects and scenes. However, these models are still susceptible to failure in the presence of execution-time impediments such as distractors and physical obstructions in the robot's workspace. Existing policy improvement methods finetune base VLAs to improve generalization, yet they still struggle in unseen distractor settings. To address this problem, we investigate whether internet-scale pretraining of large vision-language models (VLMs) can be leveraged to reason about these impediments and mitigate policy failures. To this end, we propose StageCraft, a training-free approach to improve pretrained VLA policy performance by manipulating the environment's initial state using VLM-based in-context reasoning. StageCraft takes policy rollout videos and success labels as input and leverages VLM's reasoning ability to infer which objects in the initial state need to be manipulated to avoid anticipated execution failures. StageCraft is an extensible plug-and-play module that does not introduce additional constraints on the underlying policy, and only requires a few policy rollouts to work. We evaluate performance of state-of-the-art VLA models with StageCraft and show an absolute 40% performance improvement across three real world task domains involving diverse distractors and obstructions. Our simulation experiments in RLBench empirically show that StageCraft tailors its extent of intervention based on the strength of the underlying policy and improves its performance with more in-context samples. Videos of StageCraft in effect can be found at https://stagecraft-decorator.github.io/stagecraft/ .

</details>


### [351] [E-SocialNav: Efficient Socially Compliant Navigation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20664)
*Ling Xiao,Daeun Song,Xuesu Xiao,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了E-SocialNav，一个用于机器人社交合规导航的高效语言模型，解决了现有基准过度关注导航成功率而忽视社交合规性，以及大规模语言模型计算效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型在机器人导航应用中存在两个主要问题：1）现有基准测试过于强调导航成功率，对社交合规性关注有限；2）大规模语言模型计算开销大，响应时间慢，能耗高，难以在资源受限的机器人平台上实时部署。

Method: 作者首先评估了GPT-4o和Claude在机器人导航中的社交合规性，然后提出了E-SocialNav模型。该模型采用两阶段训练流程：先进行监督微调，再进行直接偏好优化。尽管训练数据相对较小，但能生成社交合规的行为。

Result: E-SocialNav在生成社交合规行为方面持续优于零样本基线。在文本级语义相似度（与人类标注相比）和动作准确性方面都表现出色。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段训练流程，E-SocialNav实现了高效的社交合规导航，解决了大规模语言模型在机器人导航中的效率和实用性限制问题。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly applied to robotic navigation; however, existing benchmarks primarily emphasize navigation success rates while paying limited attention to social compliance. Moreover, relying on large-scale LMs can raise efficiency concerns, as their heavy computational overhead leads to slower response times and higher energy consumption, making them impractical for real-time deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms. In this work, we evaluate the social compliance of GPT-4o and Claude in robotic navigation and propose E-SocialNav, an efficient LM designed for socially compliant navigation. Despite being trained on a relatively small dataset, E-SocialNav consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines in generating socially compliant behaviors. By employing a two-stage training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning followed by direct preference optimization, E-SocialNav achieves strong performance in both text-level semantic similarity to human annotations and action accuracy. The source code is available at https://github.com/Dr-LingXiao/ESocialNav.

</details>


### [352] [ROI-Driven Foveated Attention for Unified Egocentric Representations in Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20668)
*Xinhai Sun,Xiang Shi,Menglin Zou,Wenlong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于感兴趣区域（ROI）的工程流程，通过前向运动学将末端执行器姿态投影到外部相机，生成运动对齐的手部中心ROI，实现跨机器人数据重用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI系统受限于物理交互数据的可用性和结构，现有VLA模型存在数据收集成本高、跨具身对齐有限、从互联网视觉数据到机器人控制迁移效果差等问题

Method: 提出ROI驱动的工程流程，引入以自我为中心、几何基础的数据表示。通过前向运动学将末端执行器姿态投影到单个外部相机，生成运动对齐的手部中心ROI，无需腕部相机或多视角系统。ROI从原始图像裁剪后调整大小，保留接触关键区域的高局部信息密度和全局上下文

Result: 开发了可重复的流程，涵盖校准、同步、ROI生成、确定性边界处理和元数据管理。得到的表示具有具身对齐和视角归一化特性，支持异构机器人间的数据重用

Conclusion: 以自我为中心的ROI作为实用的数据抽象，可实现可扩展的数据收集和跨具身学习，弥合互联网规模感知与机器人特定控制之间的鸿沟

Abstract: The development of embodied AI systems is increasingly constrained by the availability and structure of physical interaction data. Despite recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) models, current pipelines suffer from high data collection cost, limited cross-embodiment alignment, and poor transfer from internet-scale visual data to robot control.
  We propose a region-of-interest (ROI) driven engineering workflow that introduces an egocentric, geometry-grounded data representation. By projecting end-effector poses via forward kinematics (FK) into a single external camera, we derive movement-aligned hand-centric ROIs without requiring wrist-mounted cameras or multi-view systems. Unlike directly downsampling the full frame, ROI is cropped from the original image before resizing, preserving high local information density for contact-critical regions while retaining global context.
  We present a reproducible pipeline covering calibration, synchronization, ROI generation, deterministic boundary handling, and metadata governance. The resulting representation is embodiment-aligned and viewpoint-normalized, enabling data reuse across heterogeneous robots. We argue that egocentric ROI serves as a practical data abstraction for scalable collection and cross-embodiment learning, bridging internet-scale perception and robot-specific control.

</details>


### [353] [ToFormer: Towards Large-scale Scenario Depth Completion for Lightweight ToF Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20669)
*Juncheng Chen,Tiancheng Lai,Xingpeng Wang,Bingxin Liao,Baozhe Zhang,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个完整的短程ToF相机大场景深度补全框架，包括首个大场景ToF数据集LASER-ToF和传感器感知的深度补全网络，支持机器人实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ToF相机虽然设计紧凑、测量精度高，但感知范围有限，限制了其在大规模场景中的应用。现有的深度补全方法缺乏专门针对ToF的数据集，且难以泛化到ToF测量。

Method: 1) 构建多传感器平台和重建流程，收集真实世界ToF样本并创建首个大场景ToF深度补全数据集LASER-ToF；2) 提出传感器感知的深度补全网络，包含带3D-2D联合传播池化的3D分支和多模态交叉协方差注意力，能有效建模长距离关系并在非均匀ToF深度稀疏下实现高效3D-2D融合；3) 网络可利用视觉SLAM的稀疏点云补充ToF深度。

Result: 方法在实验中比次优方法降低了8.6%的平均绝对误差，同时保持轻量设计支持板载部署。在四旋翼无人机上以10Hz运行，实现了可靠的大规模建图和长距离规划。

Conclusion: 该工作为短程ToF相机在大规模场景中的应用提供了完整的解决方案，包括数据集、算法和实际部署验证，显著扩展了ToF相机的应用范围。

Abstract: Time-of-Flight (ToF) cameras possess compact design and high measurement precision to be applied to various robot tasks. However, their limited sensing range restricts deployment in large-scale scenarios. Depth completion has emerged as a potential solution to expand the sensing range of ToF cameras, but existing research lacks dedicated datasets and struggles to generalize to ToF measurements. In this paper, we propose a full-stack framework that enables depth completion in large-scale scenarios for short-range ToF cameras. First, we construct a multi-sensor platform with a reconstruction-based pipeline to collect real-world ToF samples with dense large-scale ground truth, yielding the first LArge-ScalE scenaRio ToF depth completion dataset (LASER-ToF). Second, we propose a sensor-aware depth completion network that incorporates a novel 3D branch with a 3D-2D Joint Propagation Pooling (JPP) module and Multimodal Cross-Covariance Attention (MXCA), enabling effective modeling of long-range relationships and efficient 3D-2D fusion under non-uniform ToF depth sparsity. Moreover, our network can utilize the sparse point cloud from visual SLAM as a supplement to ToF depth to further improve prediction accuracy. Experiments show that our method achieves an 8.6% lower mean absolute error than the second-best method, while maintaining lightweight design to support onboard deployment. Finally, to verify the system's applicability on real robots, we deploy proposed method on a quadrotor at a 10Hz runtime, enabling reliable large-scale mapping and long-range planning in challenging environments for short-range ToF cameras.

</details>


### [354] [Enhancing Vision-Based Policies with Omni-View and Cross-Modality Knowledge Distillation for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20679)
*Kai Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种知识蒸馏方法，将信息丰富的全景深度视觉策略的知识迁移到轻量级单目视觉策略，解决移动机器人视觉策略在场景迁移性、计算资源和传感器成本方面的三难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人上的视觉策略面临场景迁移性有限、机载计算资源受限和传感器硬件成本高的三难问题，需要一种既能保持高性能又轻量化的解决方案。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏方法，训练学生网络（单目视觉策略）不仅模仿专家（全景深度策略）的动作，还对齐其潜在嵌入表示，实现从信息丰富的全景深度策略到轻量级单目策略的知识迁移。

Result: 实验表明全景深度输入能提升场景迁移和导航性能，所提出的蒸馏方法相比仅模仿动作的策略能显著提升单目视觉策略的性能，真实世界实验验证了方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏将全景深度策略的知识迁移到轻量级单目策略，有效解决了移动机器人视觉策略的三难问题，实现了高性能、轻量化和低成本的平衡。

Abstract: Vision-based policies are widely applied in robotics for tasks such as manipulation and locomotion. On lightweight mobile robots, however, they face a trilemma of limited scene transferability, restricted onboard computation resources, and sensor hardware cost. To address these issues, we propose a knowledge distillation approach that transfers knowledge from an information-rich, appearance invariant omniview depth policy to a lightweight monocular policy. The key idea is to train the student not only to mimic the expert actions but also to align with the latent embeddings of the omni view depth teacher. Experiments demonstrate that omni-view and depth inputs improve the scene transfer and navigation performance, and that the proposed distillation method enhances the performance of a singleview monocular policy, compared with policies solely imitating actions. Real world experiments further validate the effectiveness and practicality of our approach. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [355] [Swim2Real: VLM-Guided System Identification for Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20827)
*Kevin Qiu,Kyle Walker,Mike Y. Michelis,Marek Cygan,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Swim2Real使用视觉语言模型反馈从游泳视频校准16参数机器鱼模拟器，无需手动设计搜索阶段，显著缩小模拟与现实差距


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准软体水生机器人面临三大挑战：非线性流固耦合导致参数空间混沌、简化流体模型存在持续模拟与现实差距、受控水生实验难以复现。先前工作需要三个手动定制阶段处理这种复杂性

Method: 使用视觉语言模型比较模拟和真实视频并提出参数更新，结合回溯线搜索验证步长大小。VLM反馈指导校准过程，无需手动设计搜索阶段

Result: 同时校准所有16个参数，在所有电机频率下最接近真实鱼速度（MAE = 7.4 mm/s，比次优方法低43%），五次运行中零异常种子。下游RL策略比BayesOpt校准模拟器游动距离远12%，比CMA-ES远90%

Conclusion: VLM引导的校准可以直接从视频中缩小水生机器人的模拟与现实差距，实现零样本RL转移到物理游泳器，无需手动系统识别，为水下机器人自动化通用模拟器调谐迈出一步

Abstract: We present Swim2Real, a pipeline that calibrates a 16-parameter robotic fish simulator from swimming videos using vision-language model (VLM) feedback, requiring no hand-designed search stages. Calibrating soft aquatic robots is particularly challenging because nonlinear fluid-structure coupling makes the parameter landscape chaotic, simplified fluid models introduce a persistent sim-to-real gap, and controlled aquatic experiments are difficult to reproduce. Prior work on this platform required three manually tailored stages to handle this complexity. The VLM compares simulated and real videos and proposes parameter updates. A backtracking line search then validates each step size, tripling the accept rate from 14% to 42% by recovering proposals where the direction is correct but the magnitude is too large. Swim2Real calibrates all 16 parameters simultaneously, most closely matching real fish velocities across all motor frequencies (MAE = 7.4 mm/s, 43% lower than the next-best method), with zero outlier seeds across five runs. Motor commands from the trained policy transfer to the physical fish at 50 Hz, completing the pipeline from swimming video to real-world deployment. Downstream RL policies swim 12% farther than those from BayesOpt-calibrated simulators and 90% farther than CMA-ES. These results demonstrate that VLM-guided calibration can close the sim-to-real gap for aquatic robots directly from video, enabling zero-shot RL transfer to physical swimmers without manual system identification, a step toward automated, general-purpose simulator tuning for underwater robotics.

</details>


### [356] [Characterizing the onset and offset of motor imagery during passive arm movements induced by an upper-body exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20885)
*Kanishka Mitra,Frigyes Samuel Racz,Satyam Kumar,Ashish D. Deshpande,José del R. Millán*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究开发了一种脑机接口系统，用于在康复外骨骼被动运动期间检测运动想象的开始和结束，实现功能性运动的自然控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合机器人技术和脑机接口进行运动康复具有巨大临床潜力，但挑战在于如何在康复外骨骼的器械噪声和被动运动干扰下，使用非侵入式脑机接口准确检测用户的运动意图。

Method: 招募10名参与者，在佩戴上肢外骨骼进行被动手臂运动时执行运动想象。使用脑电图信号构建解码器，检测休息与开始运动想象、维持与结束运动想象之间的转换。采用离线解码器评估和伪在线评估方法。

Result: 离线解码器评估显示，组平均开始准确率为60.7%，结束准确率为66.6%。伪在线评估能够复制这一性能，表明未来可实现可靠的在线外骨骼控制。

Conclusion: 参与者能够在佩戴外骨骼时产生高质量可靠的感觉运动节律，不受噪声或被动手臂运动影响，这为脑机接口控制辅助设备开辟了新可能性。

Abstract: Two distinct technologies have gained attention lately due to their prospects for motor rehabilitation: robotics and brain-machine interfaces (BMIs). Harnessing their combined efforts is a largely uncharted and promising direction that has immense clinical potential. However, a significant challenge is whether motor intentions from the user can be accurately detected using non-invasive BMIs in the presence of instrumental noise and passive movements induced by the rehabilitation exoskeleton. As an alternative to the straightforward continuous control approach, this study instead aims to characterize the onset and offset of motor imagery during passive arm movements induced by an upper-body exoskeleton to allow for the natural control (initiation and termination) of functional movements. Ten participants were recruited to perform kinesthetic motor imagery (MI) of the right arm while attached to the robot, simultaneously cued with LEDs indicating the initiation and termination of a goal-oriented reaching task. Using electroencephalogram signals, we built a decoder to detect the transition between i) rest and beginning MI and ii) maintaining and ending MI. Offline decoder evaluation achieved group average onset accuracy of 60.7% and 66.6% for offset accuracy, revealing that the start and stop of MI could be identified while attached to the robot. Furthermore, pseudo-online evaluation could replicate this performance, forecasting reliable online exoskeleton control in the future. Our approach showed that participants could produce quality and reliable sensorimotor rhythms regardless of noise or passive arm movements induced by wearing the exoskeleton, which opens new possibilities for BMI control of assistive devices.

</details>


### [357] [Implementing Robust M-Estimators with Certifiable Factor Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20932)
*Zhexin Xu,Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Helena Calatrava,Pau Closas,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应重加权M估计方法，利用可认证因子图优化技术解决内层加权最小二乘子问题，在机器人位姿估计和SLAM任务中实现了高质量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学和计算机视觉中的参数估计面临异常值污染和非凸优化问题的双重挑战。M估计通过鲁棒损失函数处理异常值，但会导致严重非凸问题难以全局求解。自适应重加权方案虽然实用，但其内层加权最小二乘子问题在旋转和位姿等非凸参数空间中仍然难以有效解决。

Method: 提出自适应重加权可认证估计方法：1）使用自适应重加权实现M估计；2）利用可认证因子图优化技术为内层加权最小二乘子问题提供全局最优性证书；3）仅需在光滑流形上进行快速局部优化；4）无缝集成到现有因子图软件库和工作流中。

Result: 在位姿图优化和地标SLAM任务上的实验评估表明，该方法比基于局部搜索的替代方法提供更高质量的估计，同时能够扩展到实际问题规模，具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功结合了自适应重加权M估计的鲁棒性和可认证因子图优化的全局最优性保证，为机器人学中的非凸参数估计问题提供了一种有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter estimation in robotics and computer vision faces formidable challenges from both outlier contamination and nonconvex optimization landscapes. While M-estimation addresses the problem of outliers through robust loss functions, it creates severely nonconvex problems that are difficult to solve globally. Adaptive reweighting schemes provide one particularly appealing strategy for implementing M-estimation in practice: these methods solve a sequence of simpler weighted least squares (WLS) subproblems, enabling both the use of standard least squares solvers and the recovery of higher-quality estimates than simple local search. However, adaptive reweighting still crucially relies upon solving the inner WLS problems effectively, a task that remains challenging in many robotics applications due to the intrinsic nonconvexity of many common parameter spaces (e.g. rotations and poses).
  In this paper, we show how one can easily implement adaptively reweighted M-estimators with certifiably correct solvers for the inner WLS subproblems using only fast local optimization over smooth manifolds. Our approach exploits recent work on certifiable factor graph optimization to provide global optimality certificates for the inner WLS subproblems while seamlessly integrating into existing factor graph-based software libraries and workflows. Experimental evaluation on pose-graph optimization and landmark SLAM tasks demonstrates that our adaptively reweighted certifiable estimation approach provides higher-quality estimates than alternative local search-based methods, while scaling tractably to realistic problem sizes.

</details>


### [358] [Geometrically Plausible Object Pose Refinement using Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20992)
*Anil Zeybek,Rhys Newbury,Snehal Dikhale,Nawid Jamali,Soshi Iba,Akansel Cosgun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合可微分物理模拟、可微分渲染和视觉触觉感知的多模态位姿优化方法，用于提升物体位姿估计的几何可行性和物理一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前物体位姿估计方法容易产生几何上不可行的位姿假设，特别是在灵巧操作中，估计的位姿经常与机械手相交或不在支撑面上

Method: 结合可微分物理模拟、可微分渲染和视觉触觉感知的多模态位姿优化方法，优化物体位姿的空间精度和物理一致性

Result: 模拟实验显示，当初始估计准确时，物体与机械手的相交体积误差减少73%；在高初始不确定性下减少超过87%，显著优于标准ICP基线方法

Conclusion: 实现物理现实基础且忠实于多模态传感器输入的位姿估计是迈向稳健手内操作的关键步骤

Abstract: State-of-the-art object pose estimation methods are prone to generating geometrically infeasible pose hypotheses. This problem is prevalent in dexterous manipulation, where estimated poses often intersect with the robotic hand or are not lying on a support surface. We propose a multi-modal pose refinement approach that combines differentiable physics simulation, differentiable rendering and visuo-tactile sensing to optimize object poses for both spatial accuracy and physical consistency. Simulated experiments show that our approach reduces the intersection volume error between the object and robotic hand by 73\% when the initial estimate is accurate and by over 87\% under high initial uncertainty, significantly outperforming standard ICP-based baselines. Furthermore, the improvement in geometric plausibility is accompanied by a concurrent reduction in translation and orientation errors. Achieving pose estimation that is grounded in physical reality while remaining faithful to multi-modal sensor inputs is a critical step toward robust in-hand manipulation.

</details>


### [359] [Dreaming the Unseen: World Model-regularized Diffusion Policy for Out-of-Distribution Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21017)
*Ziou Hu,Xiangtong Yao,Yuan Meng,Zhenshan Bing,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DDP通过将扩散世界模型深度集成到策略训练中，利用共享3D视觉编码器实现状态预测能力，在遇到OOD扰动时能检测到现实-想象差异并切换到内部想象轨迹，显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在视觉运动控制中表现出色，但在面对严重分布外扰动（如意外物体位移或视觉损坏）时容易灾难性失败。需要一种能主动处理OOD异常并保持鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出Dream Diffusion Policy (DDP)框架，通过共享3D视觉编码器将扩散世界模型深度集成到策略训练目标中。在推理时检测现实-想象差异，主动放弃损坏的视觉流，转而依赖内部"想象"（自回归预测的潜在动态）来安全绕过干扰。

Result: DDP在MetaWorld上达到73.8%的OOD成功率（无预测想象时为23.9%），在严重真实世界空间偏移下达到83.3%成功率（无预测想象时为3.3%）。在压力测试中，即使完全依赖开环想象，仍保持76.7%的真实世界成功率。

Conclusion: DDP通过深度集成扩散世界模型和策略训练，赋予策略强大的状态预测能力和OOD鲁棒性，能在遇到严重扰动时主动切换到内部想象轨迹，显著提升在分布外场景下的性能表现。

Abstract: Diffusion policies excel at visuomotor control but often fail catastrophically under severe out-of-distribution (OOD) disturbances, such as unexpected object displacements or visual corruptions. To address this vulnerability, we introduce the Dream Diffusion Policy (DDP), a framework that deeply integrates a diffusion world model into the policy's training objective via a shared 3D visual encoder. This co-optimization endows the policy with robust state-prediction capabilities. When encountering sudden OOD anomalies during inference, DDP detects the real-imagination discrepancy and actively abandons the corrupted visual stream. Instead, it relies on its internal "imagination" (autoregressively forecasted latent dynamics) to safely bypass the disruption, generating imagined trajectories before smoothly realigning with physical reality. Extensive evaluations demonstrate DDP's exceptional resilience. Notably, DDP achieves a 73.8% OOD success rate on MetaWorld (vs. 23.9% without predictive imagination) and an 83.3% success rate under severe real-world spatial shifts (vs. 3.3% without predictive imagination). Furthermore, as a stress test, DDP maintains a 76.7% real-world success rate even when relying entirely on open-loop imagination post-initialization.

</details>


### [360] [Cortical Policy: A Dual-Stream View Transformer for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21051)
*Xuening Zhang,Qi Lv,Xiang Deng,Miao Zhang,Xingbo Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cortical Policy是一个受人类大脑皮层启发的双流视觉变换器，通过静态视图流增强空间理解，动态视图流实现自适应调整，在机器人操作任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常以视图特定的方式提取静态视觉表示，导致3D空间推理能力不足和缺乏动态适应性。受人类大脑整合静态和动态视图的启发，需要开发能够同时处理空间复杂性和动态变化的机器人操作框架。

Method: 提出Cortical Policy双流视图变换器：1) 静态视图流通过预训练的3D基础模型提取几何一致关键点特征来增强空间理解；2) 动态视图流通过以自我为中心的注视估计模型的位置感知预训练，计算上复制人类皮层背侧通路；3) 将两个流的互补视图表示集成以确定最终动作。

Result: 在RLBench、具有挑战性的COLOSSEUM基准测试和真实世界任务上的实证评估表明，Cortical Policy显著优于最先进的基线方法，验证了双流设计在视觉运动控制中的优越性。

Conclusion: 这种受皮层启发的框架为机器人操作提供了新的视角，在基于视觉的机器人控制中具有更广泛的应用潜力。双流设计能够有效处理空间复杂和动态变化的任务，特别是在语言条件下。

Abstract: View transformers process multi-view observations to predict actions and have shown impressive performance in robotic manipulation. Existing methods typically extract static visual representations in a view-specific manner, leading to inadequate 3D spatial reasoning ability and a lack of dynamic adaptation. Taking inspiration from how the human brain integrates static and dynamic views to address these challenges, we propose Cortical Policy, a novel dual-stream view transformer for robotic manipulation that jointly reasons from static-view and dynamic-view streams. The static-view stream enhances spatial understanding by aligning features of geometrically consistent keypoints extracted from a pretrained 3D foundation model. The dynamic-view stream achieves adaptive adjustment through position-aware pretraining of an egocentric gaze estimation model, computationally replicating the human cortical dorsal pathway. Subsequently, the complementary view representations of both streams are integrated to determine the final actions, enabling the model to handle spatially-complex and dynamically-changing tasks under language conditions. Empirical evaluations on RLBench, the challenging COLOSSEUM benchmark, and real-world tasks demonstrate that Cortical Policy outperforms state-of-the-art baselines substantially, validating the superiority of dual-stream design for visuomotor control. Our cortex-inspired framework offers a fresh perspective for robotic manipulation and holds potential for broader application in vision-based robot control.

</details>


### [361] [CounterScene: Counterfactual Causal Reasoning in Generative World Models for Safety-Critical Closed-Loop Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21104)
*Bowen Jing,Ruiyang Hao,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CounterScene：基于因果对抗推理的安全关键驾驶场景生成框架，通过结构化反事实干预提升对抗性和真实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成方法依赖启发式对抗代理选择和随机扰动，缺乏对交互依赖的显式建模，导致真实性与对抗性之间的权衡问题。需要理解危险交互产生的原因而不仅仅是强制碰撞。

Method: 1) 因果对抗代理识别：识别关键代理并分类冲突类型；2) 冲突感知交互世界模型：使用因果交互图显式建模动态代理间依赖；3) 阶段自适应反事实引导：对识别出的代理进行最小干预，移除其时空安全边际，让风险通过自然交互传播出现

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，CounterScene在保持轨迹真实性的同时达到最强对抗效果，长时域碰撞率从基线12.3%提升至22.7%，ADE指标更优（1.88 vs 2.09）。优势随推演时长增加而扩大，并在nuPlan上实现零样本泛化，达到最先进的真实性。

Conclusion: CounterScene通过结构化反事实推理框架，在安全关键场景生成中实现了真实性与对抗性的双重提升，为自动驾驶安全测试提供了更有效的场景生成方法。

Abstract: Generating safety-critical driving scenarios requires understanding why dangerous interactions arise, rather than merely forcing collisions. However, existing methods rely on heuristic adversarial agent selection and unstructured perturbations, lacking explicit modeling of interaction dependencies and thus exhibiting a realism--adversarial trade-off. We present CounterScene, a framework that endows closed-loop generative BEV world models with structured counterfactual reasoning for safety-critical scenario generation. Given a safe scene, CounterScene asks: what if the causally critical agent had behaved differently? To answer this, we introduce causal adversarial agent identification to identify the critical agent and classify conflict types, and develop a conflict-aware interactive world model in which a causal interaction graph is used to explicitly model dynamic inter-agent dependencies. Building on this structure, stage-adaptive counterfactual guidance performs minimal interventions on the identified agent, removing its spatial and temporal safety margins while allowing risk to emerge through natural interaction propagation. Extensive experiments on nuScenes demonstrate that CounterScene achieves the strongest adversarial effectiveness while maintaining superior trajectory realism across all horizons, improving long-horizon collision rate from 12.3% to 22.7% over the strongest baseline with better realism (ADE 1.88 vs.2.09). Notably, this advantage further widens over longer rollouts, and CounterScene generalizes zero-shot to nuPlan with state-of-the-art realism.

</details>


### [362] [VisFly-Lab: Unified Differentiable Framework for First-Order Reinforcement Learning of Quadrotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21123)
*Fanxing Li,Fangyu Sun,Tianbao Zhang,Shuyu Wu,Dexin Zuo,yufei Yan,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个统一的可微分框架用于多任务四旋翼控制，并开发了改进的反向传播算法ABPT来解决训练中的状态覆盖和梯度偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可微分仿真的强化学习在四旋翼控制方面进展分散，缺乏系统性的开发和评估框架。需要统一的平台来支持多任务控制，并解决实际训练中的瓶颈问题。

Method: 1) 构建统一的可微分框架，支持悬停、跟踪、降落和竞速四个代表性任务；2) 提出ABPT算法，结合可微分滚动优化、基于价值的辅助目标和访问状态初始化来改善训练鲁棒性。

Result: ABPT在部分不可微分奖励的任务中表现最佳，在完全可微分设置中也保持竞争力。初步的真实世界部署验证了从仿真学习到的策略具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个系统化的四旋翼控制框架，并通过ABPT算法解决了实际训练瓶颈，为可微分强化学习在机器人控制中的应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: First-order reinforcement learning with differentiable simulation is promising for quadrotor control, but practical progress remains fragmented across task-specific settings. To support more systematic development and evaluation, we present a unified differentiable framework for multi-task quadrotor control. The framework is wrapped, extensible, and equipped with deployment-oriented dynamics, providing a common interface across four representative tasks: hovering, tracking, landing, and racing. We also present the suite of first-order learning algorithms, where we identify two practical bottlenecks of standard first-order training: limited state coverage caused by horizon initialization and gradient bias caused by partially non-differentiable rewards. To address these issues, we propose Amended Backpropagation Through Time (ABPT), which combines differentiable rollout optimization, a value-based auxiliary objective, and visited-state initialization to improve training robustness. Experimental results show that ABPT yields the clearest gains in tasks with partially non-differentiable rewards, while remaining competitive in fully differentiable settings. We further provide proof-of-concept real-world deployments showing initial transferability of policies learned in the proposed framework beyond simulation.

</details>


### [363] [Anatomical Prior-Driven Framework for Autonomous Robotic Cardiac Ultrasound Standard View Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21134)
*Zhiyan Cao,Zhengxi Wu,Yiwei Wang,Pei-Hsuan Lin,Li Zhang,Zhen Xie,Huan Zhao,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于解剖先验的框架，结合心脏结构分割与自主探头调整，用于心脏超声标准视图获取，通过空间关系图模块增强分割，并用强化学习实现探头自动调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声诊断依赖操作者获取标准视图，现有分割模型在纹理差异小的图像中产生解剖不一致结果，而自主探头调整方法要么依赖简单启发式规则，要么是黑盒学习。

Method: 1) 设计YOLO-based多类分割模型，加入空间关系图模块将解剖先验嵌入特征金字塔；2) 提取标准视图的量化解剖特征，拟合高斯分布构建概率解剖先验；3) 将机器人超声扫描的探头调整过程形式化为强化学习问题，状态基于实时解剖特征，奖励反映解剖先验匹配度。

Result: SRG-YOLOv11s在Special Case数据集上mAP50提升11.3%，mIoU提升6.8%；强化学习智能体在仿真中成功率92.5%，在体模实验中成功率86.7%。

Conclusion: 提出的解剖先验驱动框架有效整合了心脏结构分割与自主探头调整，提高了分割准确性和标准视图获取成功率，为自动化心脏超声诊断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cardiac ultrasound diagnosis is critical for cardiovascular disease assessment, but acquiring standard views remains highly operator-dependent. Existing medical segmentation models often yield anatomically inconsistent results in images with poor textural differentiation between distinct feature classes, while autonomous probe adjustment methods either rely on simplistic heuristic rules or black-box learning. To address these issues, our study proposed an anatomical prior (AP)-driven framework integrating cardiac structure segmentation and autonomous probe adjustment for standard view acquisition. A YOLO-based multi-class segmentation model augmented by a spatial-relation graph (SRG) module is designed to embed AP into the feature pyramid. Quantifiable anatomical features of standard views are extracted. Their priors are fitted to Gaussian distributions to construct probabilistic APs. The probe adjustment process of robotic ultrasound scanning is formalized as a reinforcement learning (RL) problem, with the RL state built from real-time anatomical features and the reward reflecting the AP matching. Experiments validate the efficacy of the framework. The SRG-YOLOv11s improves mAP50 by 11.3% and mIoU by 6.8% on the Special Case dataset, while the RL agent achieves a 92.5% success rate in simulation and 86.7% in phantom experiments.

</details>


### [364] [Dynamic Control Barrier Function Regulation with Vision-Language Models for Safe, Adaptive, and Realtime Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21142)
*Jeffrey Chen,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AlphaAdj：基于视觉语言模型实时调整控制障碍函数保守性的导航框架，在保证安全的同时提升效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在动态非结构化环境中需要平衡安全性和效率，传统控制障碍函数(CBF)使用固定参数，在安全场景过于保守，在危险场景又过于宽松，需要自适应调整

Method: 使用视觉语言模型(VLM)从RGB图像估计风险标量，映射到CBF参数动态调整安全约束强度；采用几何速度感知动态上限和过时门控融合策略处理异步推理延迟

Result: 在多种静态和动态障碍场景中，AlphaAdj保持无碰撞导航，相比固定参数设置提升效率达18.5%（路径长度和到达时间），相比无上限基线提升鲁棒性和成功率

Conclusion: AlphaAdj框架成功实现了基于视觉感知实时调整CBF保守性的目标，在保证安全的同时显著提升了导航效率，解决了传统固定参数方法的局限性

Abstract: Robots operating in dynamic, unstructured environments must balance safety and efficiency under potentially limited sensing. While control barrier functions (CBFs) provide principled collision avoidance via safety filtering, their behavior is often governed by fixed parameters that can be overly conservative in benign scenes or overly permissive near hazards. We present AlphaAdj, a vision-to-control navigation framework that uses egocentric RGB input to adapt the conservativeness of a CBF safety filter in real time. A vision-language model(VLM) produces a bounded scalar risk estimate from the current camera view, which we map to dynamically update a CBF parameter that modulates how strongly safety constraints are enforced. To address asynchronous inference and non-trivial VLM latency in practice, we combine a geometric, speed-aware dynamic cap and a staleness-gated fusion policy with lightweight implementation choices that reduce end-to-end inference overhead. We evaluate AlphaAdj across multiple static and dynamic obstacle scenarios in a variety of environments, comparing against fixed-parameter and uncapped ablations. Results show that AlphaAdj maintains collision-free navigation while improving efficiency (in terms of path length and time to goal) by up to 18.5% relative to fixed settings and improving robustness and success rate relative to an uncapped baseline.

</details>


### [365] [Affordance-Guided Enveloping Grasp Demonstration Toward Non-destructive Disassembly of Pinch-Infeasible Mating Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21143)
*Masaki Tsutsumi,Takuya Kiyokawa,Gen Sako,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理仿真的抓取模板可视化方法，通过颜色梯度显示抓取质量，帮助操作员在遮挡和几何约束下选择包络抓取策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人拆卸复杂配合部件时，夹持抓取不可行，需要多指包络抓取。但视觉遮挡和几何约束使得仅依靠2D相机难以教授合适的抓取动作

Method: 提出基于物理仿真的抓取模板方法，预先生成包络抓取候选，通过颜色梯度可视化抓取质量，增强操作员感知，辅助遥操作

Result: 仿真验证了方法在不同部件上的通用性，真实机器人实验表明，基于抓取模板的视觉增强使操作员能在严重视觉和几何约束下有效选择和教授包络抓取策略

Conclusion: 该方法通过可视化抓取质量增强操作员感知，成功解决了复杂拆卸任务中包络抓取的教学难题，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Robotic disassembly of complex mating components often renders pinch grasping infeasible, necessitating multi-fingered enveloping grasps. However, visual occlusions and geometric constraints complicate teaching appropriate grasp motions when relying solely on 2D camera feeds. To address this, we propose an affordance-guided teleoperation method that pre-generates enveloping grasp candidates via physics simulation. These Affordance Templates (ATs) are visualized with a color gradient reflecting grasp quality to augment operator perception. Simulations demonstrate the method's generality across various components. Real-robot experiments validate that AT-based visual augmentation enables operators to effectively select and teach enveloping grasp strategies for real-world disassembly, even under severe visual and geometric constraints.

</details>


### [366] [Architecture for Multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicles based Autonomous Precision Agriculture Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21183)
*Ebasa Temesgen,Nathnael Minyelshowa,Lebsework Negash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于精准农业的多无人机协同架构框架，实现从任务规划到数据处理的全自主端到端解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机在精准农业中的广泛应用，需要一个结构化的抽象框架来支持各种算法的应用，同时考虑多无人机协同工作的限制条件。

Method: 将无人机在精准农业中的各种任务建模为架构框架，包括图像处理、路径规划、通信、数据采集和田间地图绘制等模块，设计支持多无人机协调协作的最小物理干预系统。

Result: 通过仿真和实地测试验证，该架构具有容错性、鲁棒性、开发者和用户友好性等优势，能够为农民提供高效的无人机部署解决方案。

Conclusion: 提出的架构框架为精准农业中的多无人机应用提供了一个完整的端到端自主解决方案，从任务规划到数据处理都能高效运行，有助于农民全面部署无人机技术。

Abstract: The use of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in precision agriculture has seen a huge increase recently. As such, systems that aim to apply various algorithms on the field need a structured framework of abstractions. This paper defines the various tasks of the UAVs in precision agriculture and model them into an architectural framework. The presented architecture is built on the context that there will be minimal physical intervention to do the tasks defined with multiple coordinated and cooperative UAVs. Various tasks such as image processing, path planning, communication, data acquisition, and field mapping are employed in the architecture to provide an efficient system. Besides, different limitation for applying Multi-UAVs in precision agriculture has been considered in designing the architecture. The architecture provides an autonomous end-to-end solution, starting from mission planning, data acquisition and image processing framework that is highly efficient and can enable farmers to comprehensively deploy UAVs onto their lands. Simulation and field tests shows that the architecture offers a number of advantages that include fault-tolerance, robustness, developer and user-friendliness.

</details>


### [367] [GAPG: Geometry Aware Push-Grasping Synergy for Goal-Oriented Manipulation in Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21195)
*Lijingze Xiao,Jinhong Du,Yang Cong,Supeng Diao,Yu Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出几何感知的推-抓协同框架，利用点云数据整合抓取和推动评估，在杂乱环境中实现更安全高效的机器人操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中，单步抓取往往不足，需要推动作为辅助动作。现有方法忽略几何信息，导致稳定性和效率问题，需要几何感知的方法来评估抓取鲁棒性和推动动作的安全性

Method: 提出几何感知的推-抓协同框架：1) 抓取评估模块分析夹爪点云与闭合区域内点的几何关系，判断抓取可行性和稳定性；2) 推动评估模块预测推动动作如何影响未来可抓取空间；3) 联合推理几何信息，选择能将不可抓取状态转化为可抓取状态的动作

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中进行广泛测试，实验结果表明模型能很好地泛化到真实场景和未见过的物体，实现更安全、高效、可靠的杂乱环境操作

Conclusion: 通过几何感知的推-抓协同框架，利用点云数据联合推理抓取和推动的几何信息，能够在杂乱环境中实现更安全、高效、可靠的机器人操作，并具有良好的泛化能力

Abstract: Grasping target objects is a fundamental skill for robotic manipulation, but in cluttered environments with stacked or occluded objects, a single-step grasp is often insufficient. To address this, previous work has introduced pushing as an auxiliary action to create graspable space. However, these methods often struggle with both stability and efficiency because they neglect the scene's geometric information, which is essential for evaluating grasp robustness and ensuring that pushing actions are safe and effective. To this end, we propose a geometry-aware push-grasp synergy framework that leverages point cloud data to integrate grasp and push evaluation. Specifically, the grasp evaluation module analyzes the geometric relationship between the gripper's point cloud and the points enclosed within its closing region to determine grasp feasibility and stability. Guided by this, the push evaluation module predicts how pushing actions influence future graspable space, enabling the robot to select actions that reliably transform non-graspable states into graspable ones. By jointly reasoning about geometry in both grasping and pushing, our framework achieves safer, more efficient, and more reliable manipulation in cluttered settings. Our method is extensively tested in simulation and real-world environments in various scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our model generalizes well to real-world scenes and unseen objects.

</details>


### [368] [Evaluating Factor-Wise Auxiliary Dynamics Supervision for Latent Structure and Robustness in Simulated Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21268)
*Chayanin Chamachot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估因子级辅助动力学监督在模拟人形机器人运动中的效果，发现该方法既未产生有用的潜在结构，也未显著提升鲁棒性，LSTM基线表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究因子级辅助动力学监督是否能在强化学习中产生可解释的潜在结构或提升鲁棒性，特别是在人形机器人运动控制任务中。

Method: 提出DynaMITE方法，使用带tanh瓶颈的Transformer编码器，在PPO训练期间加入因子级辅助损失。在Unitree G1人形机器人的四个Isaac Lab任务上，与LSTM、Transformer和MLP基线进行比较，并进行2x2因子消融实验分析瓶颈和辅助损失的作用。

Result: 辅助监督未产生可解码的因子结构（探针R²≈0，解缠指标接近零），对奖励和鲁棒性无显著影响（ID/OOD奖励变化<0.05）。tanh瓶颈带来小幅优势（ID:+0.16，OOD:+0.10）。LSTM在所有任务上获得最佳名义奖励，DynaMITE在组合扰动下性能下降较少（2.3% vs 16.7%），但这归因于瓶颈压缩而非辅助监督。

Conclusion: 辅助动力学监督既未产生可解释的估计器，也未在奖励或鲁棒性上带来超越瓶颈压缩的显著改进。对于运动控制任务，循环网络基线仍是更优选择。

Abstract: We evaluate whether factor-wise auxiliary dynamics supervision produces useful latent structure or improved robustness in simulated humanoid locomotion. DynaMITE -- a transformer encoder with a factored 24-d latent trained by per-factor auxiliary losses during proximal policy optimization (PPO) -- is compared against Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), plain Transformer, and Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) baselines on a Unitree G1 humanoid across four Isaac Lab tasks. The supervised latent shows no evidence of decodable or functionally separable factor structure: probe R^2 ~ 0 for all five dynamics factors, clamping any subspace changes reward by < 0.05, and standard disentanglement metrics (MIG, DCI, SAP) are near zero. An unsupervised LSTM hidden state achieves higher probe R^2 (up to 0.10). A 2x2 factorial ablation (n = 10 seeds) isolates the contributions of the tanh bottleneck and auxiliary losses: the auxiliary losses show no measurable effect on either in-distribution (ID) reward (+0.03, p = 0.732) or severe out-of-distribution (OOD) reward (+0.03, p = 0.669), while the bottleneck shows a small, consistent advantage in both regimes (ID: +0.16, p = 0.207; OOD: +0.10, p = 0.208). The bottleneck advantage persists under severe combined perturbation but does not amplify, indicating a training-time representation benefit rather than a robustness mechanism. LSTM achieves the best nominal reward on all four tasks (p < 0.03); DynaMITE degrades less under combined-shift stress (2.3% vs. 16.7%), but this difference is attributable to the bottleneck compression, not the auxiliary supervision. For locomotion practitioners: auxiliary dynamics supervision does not produce an interpretable estimator and does not measurably improve reward or robustness beyond what the bottleneck alone provides; recurrent baselines remain the stronger choice for nominal performance.

</details>


### [369] [DyGeoVLN: Infusing Dynamic Geometry Foundation Model into Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21269)
*Xiangchen Liu,Hanghan Zheng,Jeil Jeong,Minsung Yoon,Lin Zhao,Zhide Zhong,Haoang Li,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DyGeoVLN：一种动态几何感知的视觉语言导航框架，通过动态几何基础模型和自适应token剪枝策略，在动态环境中实现高效导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法大多基于静态场景假设，难以泛化到动态、真实的现实世界场景中，需要解决动态环境下的导航挑战

Method: 1. 将动态几何基础模型融入VLN框架，通过跨分支特征融合实现显式3D空间表示和视觉语义推理；2. 提出无姿态自适应分辨率token剪枝策略，压缩长时程动态导航中的历史token信息，去除时空冗余token

Result: 在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能，在真实世界环境中表现出强大的鲁棒性，同时通过token剪枝降低了推理成本

Conclusion: DyGeoVLN通过动态几何感知和高效token管理，有效解决了动态环境下的视觉语言导航问题，为现实世界应用提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Vision-language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to understand visual observations and language instructions to navigate in unseen environments. Most existing approaches rely on static scene assumptions and struggle to generalize in dynamic, real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose DyGeoVLN, a dynamic geometry-aware VLN framework. Our method infuses a dynamic geometry foundation model into the VLN framework through cross-branch feature fusion to enable explicit 3D spatial representation and visual-semantic reasoning. To efficiently compress historical token information in long-horizon, dynamic navigation, we further introduce a novel pose-free and adaptive-resolution token-pruning strategy. This strategy can remove spatio-temporal redundant tokens to reduce inference cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks and exhibits strong robustness in real-world environments.

</details>


### [370] [Bayesian Active Object Recognition and 6D Pose Estimation from Multimodal Contact Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21410)
*Haodong Zheng,Gabriele M. Caddeo,Andrei C. Jalba,Wijnand A. IJsselsteijn,Lorenzo Natale,Raymond H. Cuijpers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出主动触觉探索框架，结合力/力矩传感、GelSight触觉传感和自由空间约束，通过贝叶斯推理实现物体识别和6D位姿估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决物体识别和6D位姿估计中的模糊性问题，通过主动触觉探索结合接触和非接触信息来提高鲁棒性和准确性

Method: 集成手腕力/力矩传感、GelSight触觉传感和自由空间约束于贝叶斯推理框架，使用定制粒子滤波器进行高效推理，并开发运动规划控制框架进行主动探索

Result: 在11个YCB物体上评估显示，结合触觉和自由空间信息显著提高了识别和位姿估计的准确性和稳定性，同时减少了动作周期数

Conclusion: 提出的主动触觉探索框架通过多模态传感融合和贝叶斯推理有效解决了联合物体识别和位姿估计问题，优于仅使用力/力矩传感的基线方法

Abstract: We present an active tactile exploration framework for joint object recognition and 6D pose estimation. The proposed method integrates wrist force/torque sensing, GelSight tactile sensing, and free-space constraints within a Bayesian inference framework that maintains a belief over object class and pose during active tactile exploration. By combining contact and non-contact evidence, the framework reduces ambiguity and improves robustness in the joint class-pose estimation problem. To enable efficient inference in the large hypothesis space, we employ a customized particle filter that progressively samples particles based on new observations. The inferred belief is further used to guide active exploration by selecting informative next touches under reachability constraints. For effective data collection, a motion planning and control framework is developed to plan and execute feasible paths for tactile exploration, handle unexpected contacts and GelSight-surface alignment with tactile servoing. We evaluate the framework in simulation and on a Franka Panda robot using 11 YCB objects. Results show that incorporating tactile and free-space information substantially improves recognition and pose estimation accuracy and stability, while reducing the number of action cycles compared with force/torque-only baselines. Code, dataset, and supplementary material will be made available online.

</details>


### [371] [HyReach: Vision-Guided Hybrid Manipulator Reaching in Unseen Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21421)
*Shivani Kamtikar,Kendall Koe,Justin Wasserman,Samhita Marri,Benjamin Walt,Naveen Kumar Uppalapati,Girish Krishnan,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种实时混合刚柔连续体机械臂系统，用于在非结构化、杂乱环境中实现鲁棒的开放世界物体抓取


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人系统越来越多地在非结构化、杂乱且未见过的环境中操作，需要结合柔顺性、适应性和精确控制的机械臂。传统刚性机械臂在复杂环境中缺乏适应性，而纯软体机械臂则精度不足。

Method: 系统集成了基于视觉的感知和3D场景重建，结合形状感知运动规划生成安全轨迹。采用学习型控制器驱动混合机械臂到达任意目标姿态，利用软体段的灵活性同时保持刚性段的精度。系统无需针对特定环境重新训练。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，系统在各种杂乱设置中表现出稳定的抓取性能，误差低于2厘米，展示了在非结构化环境中自适应和可靠操作的潜力。

Conclusion: 混合刚柔连续体机械臂系统能够有效应对非结构化环境中的挑战，结合了软体机械臂的适应性和刚性机械臂的精度，为开放世界物体抓取提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: As robotic systems increasingly operate in unstructured, cluttered, and previously unseen environments, there is a growing need for manipulators that combine compliance, adaptability, and precise control. This work presents a real-time hybrid rigid-soft continuum manipulator system designed for robust open-world object reaching in such challenging environments. The system integrates vision-based perception and 3D scene reconstruction with shape-aware motion planning to generate safe trajectories. A learning-based controller drives the hybrid arm to arbitrary target poses, leveraging the flexibility of the soft segment while maintaining the precision of the rigid segment.
