The system operates without environment-specific retraining, enabling direct generalization to new scenes. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate consistent reaching performance with errors below 2 cm across diverse cluttered setups, highlighting the potential of hybrid manipulators for adaptive and reliable operation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [372] [GaussianSSC: Triplane-Guided Directional Gaussian Fields for 3D Semantic Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21487)
*Ruiqi Xian,Jing Liang,He Yin,Xuewei Qi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianSSC：一种两阶段、网格原生且三平面引导的语义场景补全方法，通过高斯锚定和Gaussian-Triplane精炼模块，在不替换体素网格或维护单独高斯集的情况下注入高斯模型的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义场景补全方法在体素-图像对齐和单目占用估计方面存在不足，需要一种既能利用高斯模型优势（如表面切向、尺度、遮挡感知不对称性）又能保持体素网格效率的方法。

Method: 1. Gaussian Anchoring：亚像素级、高斯加权的图像特征聚合，提升体素-图像对齐和单目占用估计；2. 将点状体素特征转换为学习的每体素高斯场；3. Gaussian-Triplane Refinement模块：结合局部聚集（目标中心）和全局聚合（源中心）来精炼三平面特征。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI数据集上：Stage 1占用估计提升+1.0% Recall、+2.0% Precision、+1.8% IoU；Stage 2语义预测提升+1.8% IoU和+0.8% mIoU，优于现有最佳基线。

Conclusion: GaussianSSC成功将高斯模型的优势注入体素网格表示中，通过高斯锚定和Gaussian-Triplane精炼实现了更好的体素-图像对齐和语义场景补全性能，同时保持了表示效率。

Abstract: We present \emph{GaussianSSC}, a two-stage, grid-native and triplane-guided approach to semantic scene completion (SSC) that injects the benefits of Gaussians without replacing the voxel grid or maintaining a separate Gaussian set. We introduce \emph{Gaussian Anchoring}, a sub-pixel, Gaussian-weighted image aggregation over fused FPN features that tightens voxel--image alignment and improves monocular occupancy estimation. We further convert point-like voxel features into a learned per-voxel Gaussian field and refine triplane features via a triplane-aligned \emph{Gaussian--Triplane Refinement} module that combines \emph{local gathering} (target-centric) and \emph{global aggregation} (source-centric). This directional, anisotropic support captures surface tangency, scale, and occlusion-aware asymmetry while preserving the efficiency of triplane representations. On SemanticKITTI~\cite{behley2019semantickitti}, GaussianSSC improves Stage~1 occupancy by +1.0\% Recall, +2.0\% Precision, and +1.8\% IoU over state-of-the-art baselines, and improves Stage~2 semantic prediction by +1.8\% IoU and +0.8\% mIoU.

</details>


### [373] [A Framework for Closed-Loop Robotic Assembly, Alignment and Self-Recovery of Precision Optical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21496)
*Seou Choi,Sachin Vaidya,Caio Silva,Shiekh Zia Uddin,Sajib Biswas Shuvo,Shrish Choudhary,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出机器人框架实现精密光学系统的自主构建、对准和维护，演示了从随机分布组件自主构建桌面激光腔的能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然机器人自动化已改变化学和材料科学等领域的工作流程，但高精度的自由空间光学领域仍主要依赖手动操作。光学系统对空间和角度公差要求严格，且性能受紧密耦合的物理参数控制，使得通用自动化特别具有挑战性

Method: 集成分层计算机视觉系统、优化算法和定制工具，实现激光束居中、多光束空间对准、谐振腔对准、激光模式选择等功能，并能从诱导失准和干扰中自我恢复

Result: 成功演示了从随机分布组件完全自主构建桌面激光腔，系统能够执行多种精密光学任务并实现闭环自主控制

Conclusion: 通过为高灵敏度光学系统实现闭环自主性，这项工作为跨技术领域的自主光学实验奠定了基础

Abstract: Robotic automation has transformed scientific workflows in domains such as chemistry and materials science, yet free-space optics, which is a high precision domain, remains largely manual. Optical systems impose strict spatial and angular tolerances, and their performance is governed by tightly coupled physical parameters, making generalizable automation particularly challenging. In this work, we present a robotics framework for the autonomous construction, alignment, and maintenance of precision optical systems. Our approach integrates hierarchical computer vision systems, optimization routines, and custom-built tools to achieve this functionality. As a representative demonstration, we perform the fully autonomous construction of a tabletop laser cavity from randomly distributed components. The system performs several tasks such as laser beam centering, spatial alignment of multiple beams, resonator alignment, laser mode selection, and self-recovery from induced misalignment and disturbances. By achieving closed-loop autonomy for highly sensitive optical systems, this work establishes a foundation for autonomous optical experiments for applications across technical domains.

</details>


### [374] [SafePilot: A Framework for Assuring LLM-enabled Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21523)
*Weizhe Xu,Mengyu Liu,Fanxin Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafePilot是一个分层神经符号框架，通过层次化规划、形式化验证和迭代修正，为LLM赋能的网络物理系统提供端到端安全保障，防止LLM幻觉导致的不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被集成到网络物理系统中用于规划和导航，但其容易产生"幻觉"（连贯但事实错误或上下文不合适的输出），可能导致系统执行不良或不安全行为，因此需要确保LLM赋能系统的关键属性。

Method: 提出SafePilot分层神经符号框架：1) 层次化规划器通过判别器评估任务复杂度；2) 简单任务直接交给带验证的LLM任务规划器；3) 复杂任务采用分治策略分解为子任务；4) LLM任务规划器将自然语言约束转为形式化规范并验证输出；5) 检测到违规时识别缺陷、调整提示并重新调用LLM，迭代直到生成有效计划或达到限制。

Result: 框架支持具有属性基和时序约束的LLM赋能网络物理系统，通过两个案例研究证明了其有效性和适应性。

Conclusion: SafePilot为LLM赋能的网络物理系统提供了端到端的安全保障机制，通过分层规划、形式化验证和迭代修正，有效缓解了LLM幻觉带来的安全风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), deep learning architectures with typically over 10 billion parameters, have recently begun to be integrated into various cyber-physical systems (CPS) such as robotics, industrial automation, and autopilot systems. The abstract knowledge and reasoning capabilities of LLMs are employed for tasks like planning and navigation. However, a significant challenge arises from the tendency of LLMs to produce "hallucinations" - outputs that are coherent yet factually incorrect or contextually unsuitable. This characteristic can lead to undesirable or unsafe actions in the CPS. Therefore, our research focuses on assuring the LLM-enabled CPS by enhancing their critical properties. We propose SafePilot, a novel hierarchical neuro-symbolic framework that provides end-to-end assurance for LLM-enabled CPS according to attribute-based and temporal specifications. Given a task and its specification, SafePilot first invokes a hierarchical planner with a discriminator that assesses task complexity. If the task is deemed manageable, it is passed directly to an LLM-based task planner with built-in verification. Otherwise, the hierarchical planner applies a divide-and-conquer strategy, decomposing the task into sub-tasks, each of which is individually planned and later merged into a final solution. The LLM-based task planner translates natural language constraints into formal specifications and verifies the LLM's output against them. If violations are detected, it identifies the flaw, adjusts the prompt accordingly, and re-invokes the LLM. This iterative process continues until a valid plan is produced or a predefined limit is reached. Our framework supports LLM-enabled CPS with both attribute-based and temporal constraints. Its effectiveness and adaptability are demonstrated through two illustrative case studies.

</details>


### [375] [Auction-Based Task Allocation with Energy-Conscientious Trajectory Optimization for AMR Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21545)
*Jiachen Li,Soovadeep Bakshi,Jian Chu,Shihao Li,Dongmei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层两阶段框架用于多机器人任务分配与轨迹优化：基于顺序拍卖的任务分配和基于物理电池模型的能量最优轨迹规划，通过事件触发重调度处理故障和优先级变化，在505个场景中实现11.8%的平均能量节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人系统中，如何在非对称任务空间中高效分配任务并优化轨迹，同时考虑能量消耗、碰撞避免和动态变化（如机器人故障、优先级任务到达）是一个重要挑战。现有方法往往单独处理任务分配和轨迹优化，缺乏对能量消耗和物理约束的综合考虑。

Method: 1. 分层两阶段框架：第一阶段使用顺序拍卖进行任务分配，采用闭式投标函数；第二阶段每个机器人独立求解基于物理电池模型的能量最优控制问题，并通过成对邻近惩罚进行碰撞避免细化。2. 事件触发重调度机制：以有界触发频率处理机器人故障、优先级任务到达和能量偏差，使用热启动策略。

Result: 在505个场景（2-20个机器人，最多100个任务，三种工厂布局）中：1. 能量和距离两种拍卖变体相比最近任务分配平均节省11.8%能量；2. 重调度延迟低于10ms；3. 投标指标性能具有区域依赖性：在均匀工作空间中，距离投标比能量投标性能高3.5%；当摩擦异质性足够时（能量-距离相关性r<0.85），区域感知能量投标比距离投标性能高2-2.4%。

Conclusion: 投标指标的选择取决于工作空间特性：在接近均匀的地形中使用距离投标，在摩擦变化显著时使用能量感知投标。该框架为实践者提供了明确的指导，能够在动态环境中实现高效的多机器人任务分配和轨迹优化。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical two-stage framework for multi-robot task allocation and trajectory optimization in asymmetric task spaces: (1) a sequential auction allocates tasks using closed-form bid functions, and (2) each robot independently solves an optimal control problem for energy-minimal trajectories with a physics-based battery model, followed by a collision avoidance refinement step using pairwise proximity penalties. Event-triggered warm-start rescheduling with bounded trigger frequency handles robot faults, priority arrivals, and energy deviations. Across 505 scenarios with 2-20 robots and up to 100 tasks on three factory layouts, both energy- and distance-based auction variants achieve 11.8% average energy savings over nearest-task allocation, with rescheduling latency under 10 ms. The central finding is that bid-metric performance is regime-dependent: in uniform workspaces, distance bids outperform energy bids by 3.5% (p < 0.05, Wilcoxon) because a 15.7% closed-form approximation error degrades bid ranking accuracy to 87%; however, when workspace friction heterogeneity is sufficient (r < 0.85 energy-distance correlation), a zone-aware energy bid outperforms distance bids by 2-2.4%. These results provide practitioner guidance: use distance bids in near-uniform terrain and energy-aware bids when friction variation is significant.

</details>


### [376] [Conformal Koopman for Embedded Nonlinear Control with Statistical Robustness: Theory and Real-World Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21580)
*Koki Hirano,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Koopman算子的数据驱动控制框架，结合保形预测提供状态跟踪误差的概率边界，确保非线性系统在建模不确定性下的安全性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Koopman-based控制方法缺乏对建模不确定性的形式化保证，特别是同时考虑正向和逆向建模误差。需要一种分布无关的概率边界来确保非线性系统的安全控制。

Method: 结合Koopman算子与收缩理论，建立线性嵌入的离散时间非线性系统模型。采用保形预测（conformal prediction）推导状态依赖的建模不确定性边界，构建闭环控制架构，显式处理正向和逆向建模误差。

Result: 在Dubins car数值仿真和高度非线性扑翼无人机实验中验证，方法提供了形式化安全保证，同时在Koopman建模不确定性下保持精确跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为任意Koopman-based控制提供了量化性能增强手段，通过分布无关的概率边界确保了非线性系统在建模不确定性下的安全鲁棒控制。

Abstract: We propose a fully data-driven, Koopman-based framework for statistically robust control of discrete-time nonlinear systems with linear embeddings. Establishing a connection between the Koopman operator and contraction theory, it offers distribution-free probabilistic bounds on the state tracking error under Koopman modeling uncertainty. Conformal prediction is employed here to rigorously derive a bound on the state-dependent modeling uncertainty throughout the trajectory, ensuring safety and robustness without assuming a specific error prediction structure or distribution. Unlike prior approaches that merely combine conformal prediction with Koopman-based control in an open-loop setting, our method establishes a closed-loop control architecture with formal guarantees that explicitly account for both forward and inverse modeling errors. Also, by expressing the tracking error bound in terms of the control parameters and the modeling errors, our framework offers a quantitative means to formally enhance the performance of arbitrary Koopman-based control. We validate our method both in numerical simulations with the Dubins car and in real-world experiments with a highly nonlinear flapping-wing drone. The results demonstrate that our method indeed provides formal safety guarantees while maintaining accurate tracking performance under Koopman modeling uncertainty.

</details>


### [377] [RTD-RAX: Fast, Safe Trajectory Planning for Systems under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21635)
*Evanns Morales-Cuadrado,Long Kiu Chung,Shreyas Kousik,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RTD-RAX：一种运行时保障的轨迹设计扩展，通过非保守RTD生成目标导向轨迹，利用混合单调可达性进行快速在线安全认证，并在不安全时修复轨迹保证安全


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RTD存在两个关键限制：1）最坏情况可达集过近似导致的保守性；2）无法在执行时考虑实时扰动。需要一种既能快速生成轨迹，又能在线处理实时扰动的安全规划框架

Method: 1）使用非保守RTD公式快速生成目标导向候选轨迹；2）利用混合单调可达性进行快速、扰动感知的在线安全认证；3）当轨迹在实时不确定性下无法通过安全认证时，采用修复程序找到附近的安全轨迹，在保证安全的同时保持向目标前进

Result: RTD-RAX框架能够：1）减少保守性，更高效地生成轨迹；2）实时处理扰动，提供在线安全保证；3）在不安全情况下修复轨迹，确保安全的同时保持目标导向

Conclusion: RTD-RAX通过结合非保守RTD轨迹生成和混合单调可达性在线安全认证，解决了标准RTD的保守性和无法处理实时扰动的问题，为实时轨迹规划提供了更高效、更安全的解决方案

Abstract: Reachability-based Trajectory Design (RTD) is a provably safe, real-time trajectory planning framework that combines offline reachable-set computation with online trajectory optimization. However, standard RTD implementations suffer from two key limitations: conservatism induced by worst-case reachable-set overapproximations, and an inability to account for real-time disturbances during execution. This paper presents RTD-RAX, a runtime-assurance extension of RTD that utilizes a non-conservative RTD formulation to rapidly generate goal-directed candidate trajectories, and utilizes mixed monotone reachability for fast, disturbance-aware online safety certification. When proposed trajectories fail safety certification under real-time uncertainty, a repair procedure finds nearby safe trajectories that preserve progress toward the goal while guaranteeing safety under real-time disturbances.

</details>


### [378] [PRM-as-a-Judge: A Dense Evaluation Paradigm for Fine-Grained Robotic Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21669)
*Yuheng Ji,Yuyang Liu,Huajie Tan,Xuchuan Huang,Fanding Huang,Yijie Xu,Cheng Chi,Yuting Zhao,Huaihai Lyu,Peterson Co,Mingyu Cao,Qiongyu Zhang,Zhe Li,Enshen Zhou,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PRM-as-a-Judge密集评估范式，利用过程奖励模型从轨迹视频中评估机器人策略执行质量，替代传统的二元成功率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人评估主要依赖二元成功率，将丰富的执行过程压缩为单一结果，掩盖了进度、效率和稳定性等关键质量指标。需要更密集的评估方法来揭示执行过程的细节。

Method: 提出PRM-as-a-Judge范式，使用过程奖励模型从观察序列中估计任务进度。建立OPD（结果-过程-诊断）度量系统，通过任务对齐的进度潜力形式化执行质量。定义密集评估的两个公理特性：宏观一致性（可加且路径一致的聚合）和微观分辨率（对细粒度物理演变的敏感性）。

Result: 在RoboPulse诊断基准测试中，多个轨迹训练的PRM法官在微观进度辨别方面优于判别性相似性方法和通用基础模型法官。使用PRM-as-a-Judge和OPD系统对主流策略范式进行结构化审计，揭示了仅结果度量无法发现的行为特征和失败模式。

Conclusion: PRM-as-a-Judge为机器人评估提供了密集的评估范式，能够捕捉传统二元成功率无法揭示的执行过程细节，有助于更全面地理解和改进机器人策略性能。

Abstract: Current robotic evaluation is still largely dominated by binary success rates, which collapse rich execution processes into a single outcome and obscure critical qualities such as progress, efficiency, and stability. To address this limitation, we propose PRM-as-a-Judge, a dense evaluation paradigm that leverages Process Reward Models (PRMs) to audit policy execution directly from trajectory videos by estimating task progress from observation sequences. Central to this paradigm is the OPD (Outcome-Process-Diagnosis) metric system, which explicitly formalizes execution quality via a task-aligned progress potential. We characterize dense robotic evaluation through two axiomatic properties: macro-consistency, which requires additive and path-consistent aggregation, and micro-resolution, which requires sensitivity to fine-grained physical evolution. Under this formulation, potential-based PRM judges provide a natural instantiation of dense evaluation, with macro-consistency following directly from the induced scalar potential. We empirically validate the micro-resolution property using RoboPulse, a diagnostic benchmark specifically designed for probing micro-scale progress discrimination, where several trajectory-trained PRM judges outperform discriminative similarity-based methods and general-purpose foundation-model judges. Finally, leveraging PRM-as-a-Judge and the OPD metric system, we conduct a structured audit of mainstream policy paradigms across long-horizon tasks, revealing behavioral signatures and failure modes that are invisible to outcome-only metrics.

</details>


### [379] [BiPreManip: Learning Affordance-Based Bimanual Preparatory Manipulation through Anticipatory Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21679)
*Yan Shen,Feng Jiang,Zichen He,Xiaoqi Li,Yuchen Liu,Zhiyu Li,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出协作预备操作框架，通过视觉affordance表示实现双臂协调，解决难以直接抓取或功能操作物体的任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多日常物体难以直接抓取（如平板电脑）或进行功能操作（如打开笔帽），这些任务需要双臂进行顺序、不对称的协调，其中一只手臂执行预备操作以支持另一只手臂的目标导向动作

Method: 提出基于视觉affordance的框架，首先设想最终目标导向动作，然后引导一只手臂执行一系列预备操作，以促进另一只手臂的后续操作。这种affordance中心表示实现了预期的臂间推理和协调

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的大量实验表明，相比竞争基线，该方法显著提高了任务成功率和泛化能力

Conclusion: 提出的协作预备操作框架通过affordance中心表示实现了有效的双臂协调，能够泛化到不同类别的各种物体，解决了需要理解物体语义和几何、预测空间关系以及规划长时程协调动作的挑战

Abstract: Many everyday objects are difficult to directly grasp (e.g., a flat iPad) or manipulate functionally (e.g., opening the cap of a pen lying on a desk). Such tasks require sequential, asymmetric coordination between two arms, where one arm performs preparatory manipulation that enables the other's goal-directed action - for instance, pushing the iPad to the table's edge before picking it up, or lifting the pen body to allow the other hand to remove its cap. In this work, we introduce Collaborative Preparatory Manipulation, a class of bimanual manipulation tasks that demand understanding object semantics and geometry, anticipating spatial relationships, and planning long-horizon coordinated actions between the two arms. To tackle this challenge, we propose a visual affordance-based framework that first envisions the final goal-directed action and then guides one arm to perform a sequence of preparatory manipulations that facilitate the other arm's subsequent operation. This affordance-centric representation enables anticipatory inter-arm reasoning and coordination, generalizing effectively across various objects spanning diverse categories. Extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that our approach substantially improves task success rates and generalization compared to competitive baselines.

</details>


### [380] [Can a Robot Walk the Robotic Dog: Triple-Zero Collaborative Navigation for Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21723)
*Yaxuan Wang,Yifan Xiang,Ke Li,Xun Zhang,BoWen Ye,Zhuochen Fan,Fei Wei,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TZPP是一个无需训练、无需先验知识、无需仿真的异构多机器人协作路径规划框架，采用协调者-探索者架构，在真实环境中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前异构多机器人系统通常需要大量训练、先验知识和仿真，限制了实际部署。TZPP旨在消除这些依赖，实现更实用的异构机器人协作。

Method: 采用协调者-探索者架构：人形机器人（Unitree G1）负责任务协调，四足机器人（Go2）探索可行路径，利用多模态大语言模型提供指导。

Result: 在多样室内外环境（包括障碍密集和地标稀疏场景）中测试，TZPP展现出鲁棒性、接近人类效率的规划能力，以及对未见场景的强适应性。

Conclusion: 通过消除对训练和仿真的依赖，TZPP为异构机器人协作的实际部署提供了实用路径，展示了零训练、零先验、零仿真框架的可行性。

Abstract: We present Triple Zero Path Planning (TZPP), a collaborative framework for heterogeneous multi-robot systems that requires zero training, zero prior knowledge, and zero simulation. TZPP employs a coordinator--explorer architecture: a humanoid robot handles task coordination, while a quadruped robot explores and identifies feasible paths using guidance from a multimodal large language model. We implement TZPP on Unitree G1 and Go2 robots and evaluate it across diverse indoor and outdoor environments, including obstacle-rich and landmark-sparse settings. Experiments show that TZPP achieves robust, human-comparable efficiency and strong adaptability to unseen scenarios. By eliminating reliance on training and simulation, TZPP offers a practical path toward real-world deployment of heterogeneous robot cooperation. Our code and video are provided at: https://github.com/triple-zeropp/Triple-zero-robot-agent

</details>


### [381] [Memory-Efficient Boundary Map for Large-Scale Occupancy Grid Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21774)
*Benxu Tang,Yunfan Ren,Yixi Cai,Fanze Kong,Wenyi Liu,Fangcheng Zhu,Longji Yin,Liuyu Shi,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的边界地图表示方法，仅维护映射体积的边界而非整个体积，显著降低内存消耗，支持高效的三维空间占用状态查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统占用网格地图方法在保持所有体素时需要大量内存资源，特别是在高分辨率和大型场景中。需要一种更高效的内存表示方法来支持安全关键的机器人应用。

Method: 提出边界地图表示，仅显式维护边界体素（如占用体素和前沿体素），自由和未知体素通过边界内外的体积自动表示。设计了支持高效占用状态查询的数据结构，并提出了全局-局部建图框架和更新算法。

Result: 该方法将表示从三维空间的全体积维护简化为二维空间的封闭表面维护，显著减少了内存消耗。提供了占用状态查询算法的理论分析，并实现了高效的边界地图构建和更新。

Conclusion: 边界地图是一种高效的三维环境表示方法，通过仅维护边界体素大幅降低内存需求，同时支持准确的占用状态查询，适用于大规模高分辨率机器人应用。代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Determining the occupancy status of locations in the environment is a fundamental task for safety-critical robotic applications. Traditional occupancy grid mapping methods subdivide the environment into a grid of voxels, each associated with one of three occupancy states: free, occupied, or unknown. These methods explicitly maintain all voxels within the mapped volume and determine the occupancy state of a location by directly querying the corresponding voxel that the location falls within. However, maintaining all grid voxels in high-resolution and large-scale scenarios requires substantial memory resources. In this paper, we introduce a novel representation that only maintains the boundary of the mapped volume. Specifically, we explicitly represent the boundary voxels, such as the occupied voxels and frontier voxels, while free and unknown voxels are automatically represented by volumes within or outside the boundary, respectively. As our representation maintains only a closed surface in two-dimensional (2D) space, instead of the entire volume in three-dimensional (3D) space, it significantly reduces memory consumption. Then, based on this 2D representation, we propose a method to determine the occupancy state of arbitrary locations in the 3D environment. We term this method as boundary map. Besides, we design a novel data structure for maintaining the boundary map, supporting efficient occupancy state queries. Theoretical analyses of the occupancy state query algorithm are also provided. Furthermore, to enable efficient construction and updates of the boundary map from the real-time sensor measurements, we propose a global-local mapping framework and corresponding update algorithms. Finally, we will make our implementation of the boundary map open-source on GitHub to benefit the community:https://github.com/hku-mars/BDM.

</details>


### [382] [Directional Mollification for Controlled Smooth Path Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21831)
*Alfredo González-Calvin,Juan F. Jiménez,Héctor García de Marina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出方向性磨光算子，解决传统磨光方法无法精确插值路径点的限制，同时保持解析可处理性和曲率约束，为自主系统路径生成提供统一理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磨光方法虽然计算高效且具有解析可处理性，但存在几何约束：平滑路径被限制在原始路径的凸包内，无法精确插值路径点，而任务规范或上游规划器常常要求精确插值。

Method: 提出方向性磨光算子，生成无限可微路径，严格插值指定路径点，以任意精度收敛到原始不可微输入，并通过闭式表达式满足显式曲率边界。建立包含经典磨光和方向性磨光的参数化路径生成算子族。

Result: 方向性磨光算子解决了传统方法的限制，保留了经典磨光的解析可处理性优势，同时实现了精确路径点插值，为自主系统路径生成提供了满足核心要求的解决方案。

Conclusion: 方向性磨光算子突破了传统磨光方法的几何约束，实现了精确路径点插值，同时保持了平滑性、曲率约束和解析可处理性，为从非可微规划输出生成平滑可行路径提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Path generation, the problem of producing smooth, executable paths from discrete planning outputs, such as waypoint sequences, is a fundamental step in the control of autonomous robots, industrial robots, and CNC machines, as path following and trajectory tracking controllers impose strict differentiability requirements on their reference inputs to guarantee stability and convergence, particularly for nonholonomic systems. Mollification has been recently proposed as a computationally efficient and analytically tractable tool for path generation, offering formal smoothness and curvature guarantees with advantages over spline interpolation and optimization-based methods. However, this mollification is subject to a fundamental geometric constraint: the smoothed path is confined within the convex hull of the original path, precluding exact waypoint interpolation, even when explicitly required by mission specifications or upstream planners. We introduce directional mollification, a novel operator that resolves this limitation while retaining the analytical tractability of classical mollification. The proposed operator generates infinitely differentiable paths that strictly interpolate prescribed waypoints, converge to the original non-differentiable input with arbitrary precision, and satisfy explicit curvature bounds given by a closed-form expression, addressing the core requirements of path generation for controlled autonomous systems. We further establish a parametric family of path generation operators that contains both classical and directional mollification as special cases, providing a unifying theoretical framework for the systematic generation of smooth, feasible paths from non-differentiable planning outputs.

</details>


### [383] [Sim-to-Real of Humanoid Locomotion Policies via Joint Torque Space Perturbation Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21853)
*Junhyeok Rui Cha,Woohyun Cha,Jaeyong Shin,Donghyeon Kim,Jaeheung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新的sim-to-real方法，通过状态相关的扰动注入来模拟更广泛的实际差距，相比传统参数随机化方法能更好地处理非线性不确定性和接触柔顺性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sim-to-real方法通常依赖固定有限参数集的域随机化，无法充分模拟复杂的现实差距，如非线性执行器动力学和接触柔顺性等状态相关的不确定性。

Method: 在正向仿真过程中向输入关节扭矩注入状态相关的扰动，使用神经网络作为灵活的扰动生成器来模拟复杂的现实差距，无需额外训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法使仿人机器人运动策略在仿真和实际部署中都能对复杂、未见过的现实差距表现出优越的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的状态相关扰动注入方法比传统参数随机化能更有效地模拟广泛的现实差距，显著提升了控制策略在实际部署中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel alternative to existing sim-to-real methods for training control policies with simulated experiences. Unlike prior methods that typically rely on domain randomization over a fixed finite set of parameters, the proposed approach injects state-dependent perturbations into the input joint torque during forward simulation. These perturbations are designed to simulate a broader spectrum of reality gaps than standard parameter randomization without requiring additional training. By using neural networks as flexible perturbation generators, the proposed method can represent complex, state-dependent uncertainties, such as nonlinear actuator dynamics and contact compliance, that parametric randomization cannot capture. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach enables humanoid locomotion policies to achieve superior robustness against complex, unseen reality gaps in both simulation and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [384] [Optimal Solutions for the Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles via Lazy Branch and Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21880)
*Anoop Bhat,Geordan Gutow,Surya Singh,Zhongqiang Ren,Sivakumar Rathinam,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Lazy BPRC算法解决移动目标车辆路径规划问题，通过延迟计算和连续性松弛加速求解，比基准方法快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动目标车辆路径规划问题（MT-VRP-O）需要为多个智能体规划轨迹以拦截移动目标，同时考虑时间窗口、静态障碍物、速度和容量约束。传统方法计算成本高，因为需要在移动目标之间进行无碰撞运动规划。

Method: 提出Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity (Lazy BPRC)算法：1) 采用分支定价框架，包含受限主问题和定价问题；2) 使用成本下界延迟计算真实成本；3) 通过连续性松弛方法加速凸集图上的最短路径搜索。

Result: Lazy BPRC算法比两个消融实验快一个数量级，能有效找到MT-VRP-O问题的最优解。

Conclusion: Lazy BPRC通过延迟成本计算和连续性松弛，显著提高了移动目标车辆路径规划问题的求解效率，为复杂多智能体轨迹规划提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The Moving Target Vehicle Routing Problem with Obstacles (MT-VRP-O) seeks trajectories for several agents that collectively intercept a set of moving targets. Each target has one or more time windows where it must be visited, and the agents must avoid static obstacles and satisfy speed and capacity constraints. We introduce Lazy Branch-and-Price with Relaxed Continuity (Lazy BPRC), which finds optimal solutions for the MT-VRP-O. Lazy BPRC applies the branch-and-price framework for VRPs, which alternates between a restricted master problem (RMP) and a pricing problem. The RMP aims to select a sequence of target-time window pairings (called a tour) for each agent to follow, from a limited subset of tours. The pricing problem adds tours to the limited subset. Conventionally, solving the RMP requires computing the cost for an agent to follow each tour in the limited subset. Computing these costs in the MT-VRP-O is computationally intensive, since it requires collision-free motion planning between moving targets. Lazy BPRC defers cost computations by solving the RMP using lower bounds on the costs of each tour, computed via motion planning with relaxed continuity constraints. We lazily evaluate the true costs of tours as-needed. We compute a tour's cost by searching for a shortest path on a Graph of Convex Sets (GCS), and we accelerate this search using our continuity relaxation method. We demonstrate that Lazy BPRC runs up to an order of magnitude faster than two ablations.

</details>


### [385] [IGV-RRT: Prior-Real-Time Observation Fusion for Active Object Search in Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21887)
*Wei Zhang,Ping Gong,Yujie Wang,Minghui Bai,Rongfeng Ye,Yinchuan Wang,Yachao Wang,Leilei Yao,Teng Chen,Chen Sun,Chaoqun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种概率规划框架，结合不确定性感知的场景先验和视觉语言模型在线目标相关性估计，用于动态室内环境中的物体目标导航，有效应对物体重新摆放带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决动态室内环境中物体目标导航的挑战，特别是物体重新摆放会使历史场景知识失效的问题，需要能够适应环境变化的导航方法。

Method: 采用双层次语义映射模块和实时规划器。映射模块包含基于3D场景图构建的信息增益地图（IGM）用于全局指导，以及视觉语言模型分数地图（VLM-SM）用于局部验证。规划器（IGV-RRT）结合信息增益和语义证据进行在线决策，偏向具有高先验可能性和强在线相关性的语义显著区域。

Result: 模拟和真实世界实验表明，该方法能有效减轻物体重新摆放的影响，在复杂室内环境中比代表性基线方法获得更高的搜索效率和成功率。

Conclusion: 提出的概率规划框架通过结合先验知识和在线语义验证，能够有效处理动态室内环境中的物体目标导航问题，提高了导航的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) in temporally changing indoor environments is challenging because object relocation can invalidate historical scene knowledge. To address this issue, we propose a probabilistic planning framework that combines uncertainty-aware scene priors with online target relevance estimates derived from a Vision Language Model (VLM). The framework contains a dual-layer semantic mapping module and a real-time planner. The mapping module includes an Information Gain Map (IGM) built from a 3D scene graph (3DSG) during prior exploration to model object co-occurrence relations and provide global guidance on likely target regions. It also maintains a VLM score map (VLM-SM) that fuses confidence-weighted semantic observations into the map for local validation of the current scene. Based on these two cues, we develop a planner that jointly exploits information gain and semantic evidence for online decision making. The planner biases tree expansion toward semantically salient regions with high prior likelihood and strong online relevance (IGV-RRT), while preserving kinematic feasibility through gradient-based analysis. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method effectively mitigates the impact of object rearrangement, achieving higher search efficiency and success rates than representative baselines in complex indoor environments.

</details>


### [386] [Collision-Free Velocity Scheduling for Multi-Agent Systems on Predefined Routes via Inexact-Projection ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21913)
*Seungyeop Lee,Jong-Han Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于路线约束多智能体协调的优化方法，通过调整航点通过时间而非空间重路由来解决冲突，使用可微代理轨迹模型和ADMM算法求解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化多智能体运输系统中，智能体通常必须遵循预定义路线，空间重路由不可取或不可能。需要一种在保持航点顺序和名义路线分配的同时优化协调的方法

Method: 使用可微代理轨迹模型将航点时间映射到平滑位置剖面，捕捉一阶跟踪滞后；通过密集时间网格上的距离惩罚编码安全性；采用不精确投影ADMM算法结合结构化时间更新和基于梯度的碰撞校正步骤

Result: 在随机交叉、瓶颈和基于图的网络场景中的数值实验表明，该方法能在各种拥堵水平下计算可行且时间高效的调度，在测试的瓶颈案例中比代表性分层基线获得更短的任务完成时间

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了路线约束多智能体协调问题，通过时间调度而非空间重路由实现安全高效的协调，在保持路线约束的同时优化了系统性能

Abstract: In structured multi-agent transportation systems, agents often must follow predefined routes, making spatial rerouting undesirable or impossible. This paper addresses route-constrained multi-agent coordination by optimizing waypoint passage times while preserving each agent's assigned waypoint order and nominal route assignment. A differentiable surrogate trajectory model maps waypoint timings to smooth position profiles and captures first-order tracking lag, enabling pairwise safety to be encoded through distance-based penalties evaluated on a dense temporal grid spanning the mission horizon. The resulting nonlinear and nonconvex velocity-scheduling problem is solved using an inexact-projection Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) algorithm that combines structured timing updates with gradient-based collision-correction steps and avoids explicit integer sequencing variables. Numerical experiments on random-crossing, bottleneck, and graph-based network scenarios show that the proposed method computes feasible and time-efficient schedules across a range of congestion levels and yields shorter mission completion times than a representative hierarchical baseline in the tested bottleneck cases.

</details>


### [387] [Disengagement Analysis and Field Tests of a Prototypical Open-Source Level 4 Autonomous Driving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21926)
*Marvin Seegert,Christian Oefinger,Korbinian Moller,Christoph Bank,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究评估了基于Autoware的开源自动驾驶系统在236公里混合交通中的表现，通过五级关键性框架分析了30次脱离事件，发现空间脱离率为0.127次/公里，主要问题出现在低速静态物体和交通灯场景，感知和规划故障分别占40%和26.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶系统主要基于专有系统的脱离数据评估，而开源Level 4车辆在实际长距离运行中的能力尚未得到充分探索，需要评估开源自动驾驶软件在实际交通环境中的表现。

Method: 使用基于Autoware软件栈的研究车辆在236公里混合交通中进行26次测试，采用新颖的五级关键性框架对30次脱离事件进行分类分析，计算空间脱离率并识别故障模式。

Result: 空间脱离率为0.127次/公里，脱离主要发生在低速静态物体和交通灯附近；感知故障占40%（主要因物体跟踪丢失），规划故障占26.7%（主要因停车车辆导致的运行死锁）；安全驾驶员频繁进行不必要的干预，显示缺乏信任。

Conclusion: 开源软件支持广泛操作，但脱离分析对于发现标准指标遗漏的鲁棒性问题至关重要；需要改进感知和规划模块以处理静态物体和交通灯场景，同时建立驾驶员信任以减少不必要的干预。

Abstract: Proprietary Autonomous Driving Systems are typically evaluated through disengagements, unplanned manual interventions to alter vehicle behavior, as annually reported by the California Department of Motor Vehicles. However, the real-world capabilities of prototypical open-source Level 4 vehicles over substantial distances remain largely unexplored. This study evaluates a research vehicle running an Autoware-based software stack across 236 km of mixed traffic. By classifying 30 disengagements across 26 rides with a novel five-level criticality framework, we observed a spatial disengagement rate of 0.127 1/km. Interventions predominantly occurred at lower speeds near static objects and traffic lights. Perception and Planning failures accounted for 40% and 26.7% of disengagements, respectively, largely due to object-tracking losses and operational deadlocks caused by parked vehicles. Frequent, unnecessary interventions highlighted a lack of trust on the part of the safety driver. These results show that while open-source software enables extensive operations, disengagement analysis is vital for uncovering robustness issues missed by standard metrics.

</details>


### [388] [VP-VLA: Visual Prompting as an Interface for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22003)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuxin Chen,Yuqi Liu,Jinhui Ye,Pengguang Chen,Changsheng Lu,Shu Liu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VP-VLA提出了一种双系统框架，通过结构化视觉提示将高层推理与低层执行解耦，显著提升了机器人视觉-语言-动作模型的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型采用"黑盒"映射，将视觉观察和语言指令直接映射到控制信号，要求单次前向传播同时处理指令解释、空间定位和低级控制，导致空间精度差且对分布外场景鲁棒性有限。

Method: 提出VP-VLA双系统框架：1) System 2 Planner将复杂指令分解为子任务，识别目标物体和位置；2) 将这些空间锚点以结构化视觉提示（如十字准线和边界框）叠加到视觉观察上；3) System 1 Controller在训练时通过辅助视觉定位目标，生成精确的低级执行动作。

Result: 在Robocasa-GR1-Tabletop基准和SimplerEnv仿真中，VP-VLA分别将成功率提升了5%和8.3%，超越了QwenOFT和GR00T-N1.6等竞争基线。

Conclusion: 通过结构化视觉提示将高层推理与低层执行解耦的框架，显著提高了VLA模型的空间精度和鲁棒性，为解决复杂机器人任务提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically map visual observations and linguistic instructions directly to robotic control signals. This "black-box" mapping forces a single forward pass to simultaneously handle instruction interpretation, spatial grounding, and low-level control, often leading to poor spatial precision and limited robustness in out-of-distribution scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose VP-VLA, a dual-system framework that decouples high-level reasoning and low-level execution via a structured visual prompting interface. Specifically, a "System 2 Planner" decomposes complex instructions into sub-tasks and identifies relevant target objects and goal locations. These spatial anchors are then overlaid directly onto visual observations as structured visual prompts, such as crosshairs and bounding boxes. Guided by these prompts and enhanced by a novel auxiliary visual grounding objective during training, a "System 1 Controller" reliably generates precise low-level execution motions. Experiments on the Robocasa-GR1-Tabletop benchmark and SimplerEnv simulation demonstrate that VP-VLA improves success rates by 5% and 8.3%, surpassing competitive baselines including QwenOFT and GR00T-N1.6.

</details>


### [389] [MEVIUS2: Practical Open-Source Quadruped Robot with Sheet Metal Welding and Multimodal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22031)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Keita Yoneda,Shintaro Inoue,Temma Suzuki,Jun Oda,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MEVIUS2是一个开源四足机器人，尺寸与波士顿动力的Spot相当，采用金属结构，集成多种传感器，比现有开源四足机器人更耐用实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源四足机器人大多采用3D打印结构，脆弱且尺寸小；少数金属结构的也尺寸小且缺乏感知传感器，实用性不足。需要开发大型、耐用、具备感知能力的开源四足机器人。

Method: 采用钣金焊接和金属加工技术制造结构部件，减少零件数量；集成LiDAR和高动态范围相机等传感器；所有部件可通过电商服务订购；硬件、软件和训练环境全部开源。

Result: 成功开发出MEVIUS2，尺寸与Spot相当，结构耐用；能够穿越多种崎岖地形；具备环境感知能力；所有设计完全开源。

Conclusion: MEVIUS2是一个实用的大型开源四足机器人平台，通过金属结构和多传感器集成，解决了现有开源四足机器人在尺寸、耐用性和感知能力方面的不足。

Abstract: Various quadruped robots have been developed to date, and thanks to reinforcement learning, they are now capable of traversing diverse types of rough terrain. In parallel, there is a growing trend of releasing these robot designs as open-source, enabling researchers to freely build and modify robots themselves. However, most existing open-source quadruped robots have been designed with 3D printing in mind, resulting in structurally fragile systems that do not scale well in size, leading to the construction of relatively small robots. Although a few open-source quadruped robots constructed with metal components exist, they still tend to be small in size and lack multimodal sensors for perception, making them less practical. In this study, we developed MEVIUS2, an open-source quadruped robot with a size comparable to Boston Dynamics' Spot, whose structural components can all be ordered through e-commerce services. By leveraging sheet metal welding and metal machining, we achieved a large, highly durable body structure while reducing the number of individual parts. Furthermore, by integrating sensors such as LiDARs and a high dynamic range camera, the robot is capable of detailed perception of its surroundings, making it more practical than previous open-source quadruped robots. We experimentally validated that MEVIUS2 can traverse various types of rough terrain and demonstrated its environmental perception capabilities. All hardware, software, and training environments can be obtained from Supplementary Materials or https://github.com/haraduka/mevius2.

</details>


### [390] [RAFL: Generalizable Sim-to-Real of Soft Robots with Residual Acceleration Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22039)
*Dong Heon Cho,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAFL框架，通过可迁移的单元级修正动力学场增强基础模拟器，解决软机器人模拟中几何依赖效应导致的sim-to-real差距问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分模拟器在软机器人优化中存在sim-to-real差距，特别是在几何作为设计变量时更为明显。系统识别方法常将几何依赖效应吸收到材料参数中，而更复杂的本构模型计算成本过高

Method: 提出残差加速度场学习(RAFL)框架，在基础模拟器上添加可迁移的单元级修正动力学场。模型基于共享局部特征，对全局网格拓扑和离散化不敏感，通过可微分模拟器使用稀疏标记观测进行端到端训练

Result: 在sim-to-sim和sim-to-real实验中，该方法在未见形态上实现一致的零样本改进，而系统识别方法常出现负迁移。框架支持持续细化，可在形态优化过程中累积模拟精度

Conclusion: RAFL框架通过可迁移的残差场有效解决了软机器人模拟中的几何依赖效应问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提高了模拟精度和泛化能力

Abstract: Differentiable simulators enable gradient-based optimization of soft robots over material parameters, control, and morphology, but accurately modeling real systems remains challenging due to the sim-to-real gap. This issue becomes more pronounced when geometry is itself a design variable. System identification reduces discrepancies by fitting global material parameters to data; however, when constitutive models are misspecified or observations are sparse, identified parameters often absorb geometry-dependent effects rather than reflect intrinsic material behavior. More expressive constitutive models can improve accuracy but substantially increase computational cost, limiting practicality.
  We propose a residual acceleration field learning (RAFL) framework that augments a base simulator with a transferable, element-level corrective dynamics field. Operating on shared local features, the model is agnostic to global mesh topology and discretization. Trained end-to-end through a differentiable simulator using sparse marker observations, the learned residual generalizes across shapes. In both sim-to-sim and sim-to-real experiments, our method achieves consistent zero-shot improvements on unseen morphologies, while system identification frequently exhibits negative transfer. The framework also supports continual refinement, enabling simulation accuracy to accumulate during morphology optimization.

</details>


### [391] [Do World Action Models Generalize Better than VLAs? A Robustness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22078)
*Zhanguang Zhang,Zhiyuan Li,Behnam Rahmati,Rui Heng Yang,Yintao Ma,Amir Rasouli,Sajjad Pakdamansavoji,Yangzheng Wu,Lingfeng Zhang,Tongtong Cao,Feng Wen,Xingyue Quan,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较VLA与WAM在机器人动作规划中的表现，发现WAM在未见场景和抗干扰方面更具优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA方法在泛化能力和抗干扰性方面存在局限，而基于世界模型的WAM方法可能提供更好的动态预测和泛化能力

Method: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0-Plus基准测试上，对主流VLA策略和最新WAM进行对比评估，测试各种视觉和语言干扰下的表现

Result: WAM表现出更强的鲁棒性：LingBot-VA在RoboTwin 2.0-Plus上达到74.2%成功率，Cosmos-Policy在LIBERO-Plus上达到82.2%。VLA需要大量多样化训练才能达到可比鲁棒性

Conclusion: WAM因其显式动态预测能力和从网络规模视频预训练中获得的空间时间先验，在泛化能力和鲁棒性方面优于VLA。视频先验的整合方式对性能有重要影响

Abstract: Robot action planning in the real world is challenging as it requires not only understanding the current state of the environment but also predicting how it will evolve in response to actions. Vision-language-action (VLA), which repurpose large-scale vision-language models for robot action generation using action experts, have achieved notable success across a variety of robotic tasks. Nevertheless, their performance remains constrained by the scope of their training data, exhibiting limited generalization to unseen scenarios and vulnerability to diverse contextual perturbations. More recently, world models have been revisited as an alternative to VLAs. These models, referred to as world action models (WAMs), are built upon world models that are trained on large corpora of video data to predict future states. With minor adaptations, their latent representation can be decoded into robot actions. It has been suggested that their explicit dynamic prediction capacity, combined with spatiotemporal priors acquired from web-scale video pretraining, enables WAMs to generalize more effectively than VLAs. In this paper, we conduct a comparative study of prominent state-of-the-art VLA policies and recently released WAMs. We evaluate their performance on the LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0-Plus benchmarks under various visual and language perturbations. Our results show that WAMs achieve strong robustness, with LingBot-VA reaching 74.2% success rate on RoboTwin 2.0-Plus and Cosmos-Policy achieving 82.2% on LIBERO-Plus. While VLAs such as $π_{0.5}$ can achieve comparable robustness on certain tasks, they typically require extensive training with diverse robotic datasets and varied learning objectives. Hybrid approaches that partially incorporate video-based dynamic learning exhibit intermediate robustness, highlighting the importance of how video priors are integrated.

</details>


### [392] [Programming Manufacturing Robots with Imperfect AI: LLMs as Tuning Experts for FDM Print Configuration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22118)
*Ekta U. Samani,Christopher G. Atkeson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LLM引导的贝叶斯优化系统用于FDM 3D打印参数调优，相比单次AI推荐显著提升成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDM 3D打印中，打印配置对输出质量影响很大。新手通常依赖默认设置、试错或通用AI模型推荐，但这些方法无法可靠满足特定目标。专家通过迭代调整配置来获得最佳效果。

Method: 提出模块化闭环方法：将LLM作为调优专家嵌入贝叶斯优化循环中。近似评估器对每个打印配置评分并提供结构化诊断，LLM使用这些诊断提出自然语言调整建议，然后编译成机器可执行的优化指导。

Result: 在100个Thingi10k零件上测试，LLM引导的循环在78%的对象上实现了最佳配置，且0%可能失败的情况；而单次AI模型推荐很少达到最佳，且有15%可能失败的情况。

Conclusion: LLM作为约束决策模块在证据驱动的优化循环中比作为端到端的神谕式打印配置选择器提供更多价值。这一结果有望扩展到更广泛的基于LLM的机器人编程中。

Abstract: We use fused deposition modeling (FDM) 3D printing as a case study of how manufacturing robots can use imperfect AI to acquire process expertise. In FDM, print configuration strongly affects output quality. Yet, novice users typically rely on default configurations, trial-and-error, or recommendations from generic AI models (e.g., ChatGPT). These strategies can produce complete prints, but they do not reliably meet specific objectives. Experts iteratively tune print configurations using evidence from prior prints. We present a modular closed-loop approach that treats an LLM as a source of tuning expertise. We embed this source of expertise within a Bayesian optimization loop. An approximate evaluator scores each print configuration and returns structured diagnostics, which the LLM uses to propose natural-language adjustments that are compiled into machine-actionable guidance for optimization. On 100 Thingi10k parts, our LLM-guided loop achieves the best configuration on 78% objects with 0% likely-to-fail cases, while single-shot AI model recommendations are rarely best and exhibit 15% likely-to-fail cases. These results suggest that LLMs provide more value as constrained decision modules in evidence-driven optimization loops than as end-to-end oracles for print configuration selection. We expect this result to extend to broader LLM-based robot programming.

</details>


### [393] [ROBOGATE: Adaptive Failure Discovery for Safe Robot Policy Deployment via Two-Stage Boundary-Focused Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22126)
*Byungjin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROBOGATE：一个结合物理仿真和两阶段自适应采样的机器人部署风险管理框架，用于高效发现操作参数空间中的故障边界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业环境中部署学习型机器人操作策略需要进行严格的部署前验证，但在高维参数空间中进行详尽测试是不可行的。需要一种高效的方法来发现故障边界并管理部署风险。

Method: 采用两阶段自适应采样策略：第一阶段使用拉丁超立方采样（LHS）在8维参数空间中进行20,000个均匀分布的实验，建立粗略的故障景观；第二阶段应用边界聚焦采样，在30-70%成功率过渡区域集中进行10,000个额外实验，实现精确的故障边界映射。使用NVIDIA Isaac Sim进行物理仿真，评估两种机器人平台（Franka Panda和UR5e）的拾放控制器。

Result: 逻辑回归风险模型在组合数据集上达到0.780的AUC（相比第一阶段单独的0.754），识别出封闭形式的故障边界方程，并揭示了影响两种机器人平台的四个通用危险区域。在VLA模型评估中，Octo-Small在68个对抗场景中成功率为0.0%，而脚本基线的成功率为100%，显示出100点的差距。

Conclusion: ROBOGATE框架能够高效发现机器人部署中的故障边界，识别通用危险区域，并显著揭示基础模型在工业环境部署中的挑战。该框架开源且可在单GPU工作站上运行。

Abstract: Deploying learned robot manipulation policies in industrial settings requires rigorous pre-deployment validation, yet exhaustive testing across high-dimensional parameter spaces is intractable. We present ROBOGATE, a deployment risk management framework that combines physics-based simulation with a two-stage adaptive sampling strategy to efficiently discover failure boundaries in the operational parameter space. Stage 1 employs Latin Hypercube Sampling (LHS) across an 8-dimensional parameter space to establish a coarse failure landscape from 20,000 uniformly distributed experiments. Stage 2 applies boundary-focused sampling that concentrates 10,000 additional experiments in the 30-70% success rate transition zone, enabling precise failure boundary mapping. Using NVIDIA Isaac Sim with Newton physics, we evaluate a scripted pick-and-place controller on two robot embodiments -- Franka Panda (7-DOF) and UR5e (6-DOF) -- across 30,000 total experiments. Our logistic regression risk model achieves an AUC of 0.780 on the combined dataset (vs. 0.754 for Stage 1 alone), identifies a closed-form failure boundary equation, and reveals four universal danger zones affecting both robot platforms. We further demonstrate the framework on VLA (Vision-Language-Action) model evaluation, where Octo-Small achieves 0.0% success rate on 68 adversarial scenarios versus 100% for the scripted baseline -- a 100-point gap that underscores the challenge of deploying foundation models in industrial settings. ROBOGATE is open-source and runs on a single GPU workstation.

</details>


### [394] [Closed-Loop Verbal Reinforcement Learning for Task-Level Robotic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22169)
*Dmitrii Plotnikov,Iaroslav Kolomiets,Dmitrii Maliukov,Dmitrij Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Artem Trandofilov,Georgii Gazaryan,Kirill Bogatikov,Timofei Kozlov,Igor Duchinskii,Mikhail Konenkov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VRL框架，通过语言模型实现可解释的任务级规划，在移动机器人系统中实现透明策略改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决移动机器人在执行不确定性下的可解释任务规划问题，传统强化学习缺乏透明性和可解释性

Method: 采用闭环架构，使用LLM作为执行器生成行为树，VLM作为批评者提供结构化自然语言反馈，实现符号级策略更新

Result: 在真实移动机器人多阶段操作导航任务中验证，支持可解释策略改进、闭环适应执行失败、可靠物理部署

Conclusion: VRL框架实现了透明推理、显式因果反馈和人类可解释的策略演化，为机器人系统提供了新的可解释规划方法

Abstract: We propose a new Verbal Reinforcement Learning (VRL) framework for interpretable task-level planning in mobile robotic systems operating under execution uncertainty. The framework follows a closed-loop architecture that enables iterative policy improvement through interaction with the physical environment. In our framework, executable Behavior Trees are repeatedly refined by a Large Language Model actor using structured natural-language feedback produced by a Vision-Language Model critic that observes the physical robot and execution traces. Unlike conventional reinforcement learning, policy updates in VRL occur directly at the symbolic planning level, without gradient-based optimization. This enables transparent reasoning, explicit causal feedback, and human-interpretable policy evolution. We validate the proposed framework on a real mobile robot performing a multi-stage manipulation and navigation task under execution uncertainty. Experimental results show that the framework supports explainable policy improvements, closed-loop adaptation to execution failures, and reliable deployment on physical robotic systems.

</details>


### [395] [Cross-Modal Reinforcement Learning for Navigation with Degraded Depth Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22182)
*Omkar Sawant,Luca Zanatta,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出跨模态Wasserstein自编码器框架，利用深度与灰度图像的互补信息进行鲁棒导航，在深度传感器退化时仍能保持性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中，深度传感器常因光照不佳、反光表面等恶劣条件而退化，需要一种能够利用多模态互补信息进行鲁棒导航的方法

Method: 提出跨模态Wasserstein自编码器，通过强制跨模态一致性学习共享潜在表示，使系统能从灰度观测中推断深度相关特征，并与强化学习策略结合用于避障导航

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法在深度传感器显著退化时仍能保持鲁棒性能，并能成功迁移到真实环境

Conclusion: 跨模态学习框架通过利用深度和灰度图像的互补信息，实现了在传感器退化条件下的鲁棒导航，为解决实际环境中的传感器失效问题提供了有效方案

Abstract: This paper presents a cross-modal learning framework that exploits complementary information from depth and grayscale images for robust navigation. We introduce a Cross-Modal Wasserstein Autoencoder that learns shared latent representations by enforcing cross-modal consistency, enabling the system to infer depth-relevant features from grayscale observations when depth measurements are corrupted. The learned representations are integrated with a Reinforcement Learning-based policy for collision-free navigation in unstructured environments when depth sensors experience degradation due to adverse conditions such as poor lighting or reflective surfaces. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance under significant depth degradation and successfully transfers to real environments.

</details>


### [396] [Make Tracking Easy: Neural Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Whole-body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22201)
*Qingrui Zhao,Kaiyue Yang,Xiyu Wang,Shiqi Zhao,Yi Lu,Xinfang Zhang,Wei Yin,Qiu Shen,Xiao-Xiao Long,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NMR神经运动重定向框架，通过将重定向问题从优化最优解转为学习数据分布，解决人形机器人运动重定向中的关节跳跃和自碰撞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要多样化的运动技能以适应复杂环境，但从人类数据到机器人运动的重定向存在运动学和动力学上的"具身差距"。传统基于优化的重定向方法存在非凸性、容易陷入局部最优，导致关节跳跃和自碰撞等物理伪影。

Method: 提出NMR框架，包括：1) CEPR分层数据管道，利用VAE运动聚类将异质运动分组为潜在模式；2) 大规模并行强化学习专家将噪声人类演示投影到机器人可行运动流形；3) 非自回归CNN-Transformer架构，利用全局时间上下文抑制重建噪声并绕过几何陷阱。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，NMR消除了关节跳跃，显著减少了自碰撞，优于现有基线方法。NMR生成的参考运动加速了下游全身控制策略的收敛。

Conclusion: NMR通过将重定向问题重新定义为数据分布学习而非优化求解，为弥合人-机器人具身差距提供了可扩展的路径，生成的参考运动能有效提升下游控制性能。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require diverse motor skills to integrate into complex environments, but bridging the kinematic and dynamic embodiment gap from human data remains a major bottleneck. We demonstrate through Hessian analysis that traditional optimization-based retargeting is inherently non-convex and prone to local optima, leading to physical artifacts like joint jumps and self-penetration. To address this, we reformulate the targeting problem as learning data distribution rather than optimizing optimal solutions, where we propose NMR, a Neural Motion Retargeting framework that transforms static geometric mapping into a dynamics-aware learned process. We first propose Clustered-Expert Physics Refinement (CEPR), a hierarchical data pipeline that leverages VAE-based motion clustering to group heterogeneous movements into latent motifs. This strategy significantly reduces the computational overhead of massively parallel reinforcement learning experts, which project and repair noisy human demonstrations onto the robot's feasible motion manifold. The resulting high-fidelity data supervises a non-autoregressive CNN-Transformer architecture that reasons over global temporal context to suppress reconstruction noise and bypass geometric traps. Experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid across diverse dynamic tasks (e.g., martial arts, dancing) show that NMR eliminates joint jumps and significantly reduces self-collisions compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, NMR-generated references accelerate the convergence of downstream whole-body control policies, establishing a scalable path for bridging the human-robot embodiment gap.

</details>


### [397] [DexDrummer: In-Hand, Contact-Rich, and Long-Horizon Dexterous Robot Drumming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22263)
*Hung-Chieh Fang,Amber Xie,Jennifer Grannen,Kenneth Llontop,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDrummer：一种用于击鼓的层次化双手灵巧操作策略，通过结合轨迹规划和强化学习实现长时程、接触丰富的灵巧操控


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 击鼓任务自然整合了灵巧操作的三大挑战：手内控制、接触丰富的交互和长时程协调，可作为测试灵巧操作能力的理想平台

Method: 提出DexDrummer框架，采用层次化、以物体为中心的双手机器人策略，结合轨迹规划和残差强化学习修正，通过显式建模手指-鼓槌和鼓槌-鼓面交互的奖励函数训练

Result: 在仿真中能演奏两种风格音乐，灵巧策略比固定抓握策略性能提升1.87倍（简单歌曲）和1.22倍（困难歌曲）；在真实世界中能在多鼓设置下演奏，训练歌曲及其扩展版本F1得分达1.0

Conclusion: 击鼓是测试灵巧操作能力的有效测试平台，DexDrummer框架通过层次化策略和仿真到真实迁移，成功实现了长时程、接触丰富的双手灵巧击鼓任务

Abstract: Performing in-hand, contact-rich, and long-horizon dexterous manipulation remains an unsolved challenge in robotics. Prior hand dexterity works have considered each of these three challenges in isolation, yet do not combine these skills into a single, complex task. To further test the capabilities of dexterity, we propose drumming as a testbed for dexterous manipulation. Drumming naturally integrates all three challenges: it involves in-hand control for stabilizing and adjusting the drumstick with the fingers, contact-rich interaction through repeated striking of the drum surface, and long-horizon coordination when switching between drums and sustaining rhythmic play. We present DexDrummer, a hierarchical object-centric bimanual drumming policy trained in simulation with sim-to-real transfer. The framework reduces the exploration difficulty of pure reinforcement learning by combining trajectory planning with residual RL corrections for fast transitions between drums. A dexterous manipulation policy handles contact-rich dynamics, guided by rewards that explicitly model both finger-stick and stick-drum interactions. In simulation, we show our policy can play two styles of music: multi-drum, bimanual songs and challenging, technical exercises that require increased dexterity. Across simulated bimanual tasks, our dexterous, reactive policy outperforms a fixed grasp policy by 1.87x across easy songs and 1.22x across hard songs F1 scores. In real-world tasks, we show song performance across a multi-drum setup. DexDrummer is able to play our training song and its extended version with an F1 score of 1.0.

</details>


### [398] [UniDex: A Robot Foundation Suite for Universal Dexterous Hand Control from Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22264)
*Gu Zhang,Qicheng Xu,Haozhe Zhang,Jianhan Ma,Long He,Yiming Bao,Zeyu Ping,Zhecheng Yuan,Chenhao Lu,Chengbo Yuan,Tianhai Liang,Xiaoyu Tian,Maanping Shao,Feihong Zhang,Mingyu Ding,Yang Gao,Hao Zhao,Hang Zhao,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniDex是一个机器人基础套件，包含大规模机器人数据集、统一的视觉-语言-动作策略和实用的人体数据采集系统，用于通用灵巧手控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作面临三大挑战：真实机器人遥操作数据收集成本高、手部执行器异构性大、控制维度高。需要一种通用解决方案来降低数据收集成本并实现跨手部平台的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 构建UniDex-Dataset：从人类第一视角视频数据集转化50K+轨迹，覆盖8种灵巧手；采用人机协同重定向方法对齐指尖轨迹，保留合理的手-物接触；2) 提出FAAS统一动作空间：将功能相似的执行器映射到共享坐标，实现跨手部迁移；3) 训练UniDex-VLA：基于FAAS的3D视觉-语言-动作策略；4) 开发UniDex-Cap：便携式采集系统，记录同步RGB-D流和手部姿态，转化为机器人可执行轨迹。

Result: 在两个不同手部上执行工具使用任务时，UniDex-VLA平均任务完成率达到81%，大幅优于现有VLA基线方法，展现出强大的空间、物体和零样本跨手部泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDex-Dataset、UniDex-VLA和UniDex-Cap共同构成了一个可扩展的通用灵巧操作基础套件，为解决灵巧操作的数据稀缺和跨平台泛化问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation remains challenging due to the cost of collecting real-robot teleoperation data, the heterogeneity of hand embodiments, and the high dimensionality of control. We present UniDex, a robot foundation suite that couples a large-scale robot-centric dataset with a unified vision-language-action (VLA) policy and a practical human-data capture setup for universal dexterous hand control. First, we construct UniDex-Dataset, a robot-centric dataset over 50K trajectories across eight dexterous hands (6--24 DoFs), derived from egocentric human video datasets. To transform human data into robot-executable trajectories, we employ a human-in-the-loop retargeting procedure to align fingertip trajectories while preserving plausible hand-object contacts, and we operate on explicit 3D pointclouds with human hands masked to narrow kinematic and visual gaps. Second, we introduce the Function-Actuator-Aligned Space (FAAS), a unified action space that maps functionally similar actuators to shared coordinates, enabling cross-hand transfer. Leveraging FAAS as the action parameterization, we train UniDex-VLA, a 3D VLA policy pretrained on UniDex-Dataset and finetuned with task demonstrations. In addition, we build UniDex-Cap, a simple portable capture setup that records synchronized RGB-D streams and human hand poses and converts them into robot-executable trajectories to enable human-robot data co-training that reduces reliance on costly robot demonstrations. On challenging tool-use tasks across two different hands, UniDex-VLA achieves 81% average task progress and outperforms prior VLA baselines by a large margin, while exhibiting strong spatial, object, and zero-shot cross-hand generalization. Together, UniDex-Dataset, UniDex-VLA, and UniDex-Cap provide a scalable foundation suite for universal dexterous manipulation.

</details>
